### [1] [Misanthrope: A Privacy-Preserving Keypoint Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23012)
*Francesco Vultaggio,Predrag Djindjic,Markus Gerke,Sebastian Tschiatschek,Phillipp Fanta-Jende*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Misanthrope是一种通过自蒸馏训练的隐私保护关键点检测器，通过避免在人物上检测关键点来从源头缓解图像特征的逆向攻击，同时在图像匹配性能上与最先进方法持平或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部图像特征易受逆向攻击，对手可从特征重建出含隐私的（尤其是人物）场景内容。在分布式计算场景中，预计算特征离开边缘设备交由远程服务器处理时，这一威胁尤为严重。

Method: 通过自蒸馏训练一个隐私保护的关键点检测器（Misanthrope），使其避免在人物区域检测关键点，从而在源头而非事后混淆的方式缓解逆向攻击。

Result: 传统特征检测管道的逆向图像可被用于检测和重新识别人物，而Misanthrope能缓解这些攻击；同时Misanthrope保持与最先进方法相当的图像匹配性能，在人物作为干扰物的挑战场景（如phototourism和野外里程计）中甚至超越现有方法。在IMC 2021 Phototourism测试集上，Misanthrope在9个场景中的7个是最优的稀疏特征提取器。

Conclusion: Misanthrope证明通过设计隐私感知的特征检测器可以在不牺牲匹配性能的前提下从源头防御逆向攻击，为隐私保护图像匹配提供了一个有效且实用的新方向。

Abstract: Image matching is a core component of applications such as Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), Visual Localization, and Structure from Motion (SfM). However, the local image features central to this task are vulnerable to inversion attacks, which enable adversaries to reconstruct privacy-sensitive scene content from local features. These attacks pose a particular threat in distributed computing scenarios where the pre-computed features leave edge devices to be processed by remote servers. In this work, we introduce Misanthrope, a novel privacy-preserving keypoint detector trained through self-distillation to avoid detecting keypoints on people---a predominant source of privacy-sensitive content in most localization scenarios---thus mitigating inversion attacks at the source rather than through post-hoc obfuscation. We demonstrate how inverted images from traditional feature detection pipelines can be used to detect and re-identify people in the scene, while Misanthrope is able to mitigate these attacks. Furthermore, Misanthrope maintains image matching performance on par with the state of the art and even surpasses it in challenging settings where people act as distractors, such as phototourism and in-the-wild odometry. On the Image Matching Challenge 2021 Phototourism test set, Misanthrope is the top-performing sparse feature extractor in 7 out of 9 scenes. We make our model and its evaluation script available here: https://github.com/fratopa/misanthrope

</details>


### [2] [Aligning Human Sense: Calibrated Distributional Reward Learning for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21425)
*Nai-Xin Zhai,Weihua Cheng,Dexu Yu,Yikai Gu,Hanwen Du,Junchen Fu,Chenxi Huang,Yingwei Song,Liyuan Lillian Ma,Yang Ran,Youhua Li,Yongxin Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的偏好感知学习框架（AHS），用于视频生成中的人机对齐，通过精英引导过滤校准偏好数据、多维奖励分布建模捕捉不确定性、以及基于Wasserstein距离的分布对齐改进GRPO策略优化，从而提升奖励信号的可靠性和生成视频的感知一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成质量评估依赖与人类偏好的对齐，但当前存在三个关键挑战：1）奖励信号可靠性受限于含噪声和偏差的人类偏好数据；2）标量奖励模型将多维度人类偏好压缩为单一数值，丢失了偏好维度间的动态权衡；3）策略优化中广泛使用的KL散度主要施加局部约束，无法捕捉人类偏好的全局结构。

Method: 提出统一偏好感知学习框架：1) 引入精英引导过滤（elite-guided filtering）来校准偏好数据，构建可靠的奖励模型训练监督信号；2) 将视频质量建模为多维奖励分布，以捕捉人类偏好的固有不确定性，并使用Wasserstein距离对齐学习到的奖励分布与经验人类偏好分布；3) 将基于Wasserstein的分布对齐引入GRPO，引导策略优化更好地匹配人类偏好的全局结构。

Result: 在奖励建模和视频生成任务上的实验表明，该方法提升了奖励信号的可靠性以及生成视频的感知一致性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过偏好数据校准、多维奖励分布建模和Wasserstein分布对齐三个层面的协同设计，该框架有效解决了视频生成中偏好对齐的可靠性、多维度权衡和全局结构捕捉问题，验证了分布级对齐在视频生成策略优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Video generation is central to AI-powered content creation. Aligning generated videos with human preferences is a key criterion for evaluating generation quality. Despite significant progress in visual quality, three key challenges remain. First, the reliability of reward signals is constrained by the quality of human preference data, which is often affected by subjective noise and bias. Second, standard scalar reward models collapse multi-aspect human preferences into a single value, leading to the loss of dynamic trade-offs across multiple preference dimensions. Third, in policy optimization, the widely adopted KL divergence imposes primarily local constraints and may fail to capture the global structure of human preferences. To address these challenges, we propose a unified preference-aware learning framework for video generation. First, we introduce elite-guided filtering to calibrate preference data and construct reliable supervision for reward model training. We then model video quality as a multidimensional reward distribution to capture the uncertainty inherent in human preferences, and use the Wasserstein distance to align the learned reward distribution with the empirical human preference distribution. Finally, we introduce Wasserstein-based distributional alignment into GRPO, guiding policy optimization to better match the global structure of human preferences over videos. Experiments on reward modeling and video generation demonstrate that our approach improves the reliability of reward signals and the perceptual consistency of generated videos. Our code is available at https://github.com/alignhs26/ahs.

</details>


### [3] [BenthicFlow: Generating Extensible Underwater Environments via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23173)
*Joaquín Figueira,Camile Lendering,Manfred Gonzalez-Hernandez,Giacomo D'Amicantonio,Erkut Akdag,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BenthicFlow是一个基于单一条件流匹配模型的统一框架，可联合生成对齐的纹理和深度图，通过MultiDiffusion启发的采样过程生成空间可扩展的RGBD马赛克，并利用表面对齐的高斯曲面将其提升为显式3D海底环境，从而解决水下3D场景理解中数据不足和模型泛化差的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下3D场景理解面临数据缺失和地面训练模型无法泛化到水下场景的挑战。现有方法使用多个独立训练的模型拼接生成的大场景块，且只在单个调查点展示异质景观，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出BenthicFlow统一框架，采用单个条件流匹配模型联合生成对齐的纹理和深度图；使用MultiDiffusion启发的采样过程在整个生成轨迹中调和重叠窗口，无需单独的拼接模型即可生成空间可扩展的RGBD马赛克；最后通过表面对齐的高斯曲面将生成的马赛克提升为显式3D海底环境。

Result: 在多个地理上不同的调查站点进行的实验表明，BenthicFlow能够保持站点特定的外观，同时生成连贯的大规模3D场景，且与目标分布紧密匹配。

Conclusion: BenthicFlow通过统一的生成模型框架解决了水下3D场景生成中的数据域差距问题，实现了无需后处理拼接的大规模、连贯、站点特定的3D海底场景生成。

Abstract: Computer vision applications for 3D scene understanding in underwater environments remain challenging due to the lack of high-quality 3D data and the inability of surface-trained models to generalize to underwater scenes. To address this challenge, an emerging trend is to employ generative models to close the data domain gap. However, existing methods assemble large scenes by stitching independently generated tiles post hoc with separately trained models, while demonstrating heterogeneous landscapes only within individual survey sites. We introduce BenthicFlow, a unified framework based on a single conditional flow-matching model that jointly generates aligned textures and depth maps. A MultiDiffusion-inspired sampling procedure reconciles overlapping windows throughout the generative trajectory, enabling spatially extensible RGBD mosaics without a separate stitching model. The generated mosaics are subsequently lifted into explicit 3D benthic environments using surface-aligned Gaussian surfels. Experiments across geographically distinct survey sites demonstrate that BenthicFlow preserves site-specific appearance while generating coherent, large-scale 3D scenes that closely match the target distributions. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/jacomof/BenthicFlow.

</details>


### [4] [Following Motion for Sequential Modeling in Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22861)
*Jaehyun Park,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MGMVFI，一种面向视频帧插值（VFI）的任务定制化选择性状态空间模型。通过运动引导序列化（MGS）利用光流定义运动自适应的1D输入顺序，使因果状态更新与语义相关token对齐；同时引入上下文合成机制缓解不准确光流导致的特征退化。在标准VFI基准上达到SOTA，尤其在复杂动态运动场景表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态空间模型（SSM）在视频帧插值中具有线性计算复杂度下捕获长程依赖的优势，但其预定义的扫描顺序无法有效建模VFI中固有的动态运动轨迹，限制了模型在复杂运动场景下的表现能力。

Method: 1) 运动引导序列化（MGS）：利用光流定义运动自适应的1D输入顺序，使SSM的因果状态更新与语义相关的token对齐，实现运动一致的特征传播；2) 上下文合成：利用周围空间上下文进行鲁棒的帧间特征合成，缓解光流估计不准确导致的特征不可靠问题；3) 轻量级细化模块：以较低计算成本增强局部细节重建；以上组件无缝集成于定制的Mamba架构中。

Result: 在标准VFI基准上的大量实验表明，MGMVFI取得了SOTA性能，尤其在复杂和动态运动场景下表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: MGMVFI验证了将SSM选择性状态空间模型进行运动引导式改造在视频插值任务中的有效性，为序列建模在视频插值中的应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) have surfaced as a promising architecture in Video Frame Interpolation (VFI), as they can capture long-range dependencies with linear computational complexity. However, their predefined scanning order limits their effectiveness in modeling the dynamic motion trajectories inherent in VFI problems. To tackle this challenge, we propose Motion-Guided Mamba for Video Frame Interpolation (MGMVFI), an adaptation of the selective state space model tailored explicitly for VFI. MGMVFI introduces Motion-Guided Serialization (MGS), which leverages optical flow to define a motion-adaptive 1D input order for the SSM. This aligns the causal state updates with semantically related tokens, enabling motion-consistent feature propagation, particularly for large and dynamic motions. Additionally, to mitigate the unreliable feature representations caused by inaccurate optical flow estimates, we introduce contextual synthesis that utilizes the surrounding spatial context for robust inter-frame feature synthesis. These components are seamlessly integrated within our tailored Mamba architecture, which also employs a lightweight refinement block to enhance local detail reconstruction at a reduced computational cost. Extensive experiments on standard VFI benchmarks demonstrate that MGMVFI achievesstate-of-the-artperformance,particularly on complex and dynamic motions, thereby establishing a new direction for sequence modeling in video interpolation.

</details>


### [5] [Seeing the Unseen: Semantic-in-Gaussian for Sparse-View 3D Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22740)
*Zeyang Bai,Yunpeng Wang,Yunbiao Wang,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeeU是一个新颖的通用3D高斯泼溅（G-3DGS）框架，通过语义条件化的高斯空间精炼来解决稀疏视角下部分观测或遮挡区域的结构不完整问题。核心设计"语义在高斯中"包括跨视角熵感知模块和多视角条件高斯Transformer，在多个基准上显著提升渲染质量和结构完整性，尤其在具有挑战性的外推设置下平均PSNR提升2.44dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的G-3DGS框架受限于像素对齐的高斯估计，在部分观测或遮挡区域表现不佳，导致不完整的表面或结构塌陷，需要更鲁棒的方法来恢复这些欠约束区域的结构。

Method: 提出SeeU框架，核心为"语义在高斯中"的语义条件化精炼。包含两个关键模块：(1)跨视角熵感知（CEA）模块，聚合多视角语义和几何线索为紧凑嵌入；(2)条件高斯Transformer，对这些嵌入进行残差更新，恢复粗高斯中欠约束区域的结构，同时保持表面一致性。

Result: 在多个基准上的综合实验表明，SeeU一致性地提升渲染质量和结构完整性，同时保持高效的前馈推理。在具有挑战性的外推设置下，相比近期SOTA G-3DGS方法平均PSNR提升2.44dB。

Conclusion: SeeU通过语义条件化高斯精炼有效解决了G-3DGS在稀疏视角下对不可见区域结构恢复的难题，验证了将跨视角语义信息融入高斯空间进行精炼的有效性。

Abstract: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (G-3DGS) has emerged as a promising approach for novel view synthesis undersparse-view settings. However, existing frameworks remain restricted by pixel-aligned Gaussian estimation, whichstruggles in partially observed or occluded regions and often leads to incomplete surfaces or structural collapse. Toaddress these challenges, we propose SeeU (Seeing the Unseen), a novel G-3DGS framework. We frame its core design asSemantic-in-Gaussian: semantic-conditioned refinement in Gaussian space. Specifically, we introduce a Cross-viewEntropy-Aware (CEA) module that aggregates multi-view semantic and geometric cues into compact embeddings. Theseembeddings guide the Conditional Gaussian Transformer, which applies residual updates to coarse Gaussians, helpingrecover under-constrained regions of partially observed structures while preserving surface consistency. Comprehensiveexperiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SeeU consistently improves rendering quality and structuralcompleteness while retaining efficient feed-forward inference. Especially under challenging extrapolation settings,SeeU achieves an average improvement of 2.44 dB in PSNR compared to recent SOTA G-3DGS methods.

</details>


### [6] [Towards Alias-Free 4D Gaussian Representations with Motion-Aware Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21828)
*Ankit Dhiman,Kunal A Kathare,Pranav Vignesh,Lokesh R Boregowda,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向4D动态场景表示的、运动感知的3D平滑滤波器，通过估计时间与焦距-深度比的联合密度函数，自适应地调整滤波强度，有效缓解动态场景新视角合成（尤其是缩放操作）中的混叠伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景的新视角合成是AR/VR应用的关键技术，然而现有的4D表示（如动态3DGS和动态NeRF）在生成发散视角（缩放操作）时会产生混叠伪影。虽然Mip-Splatting提出的3D平滑滤波器看似可行，但它们未考虑局部运动信息，在动态场景中仍会表现出混叠问题。

Method: 提出一种运动感知的3D平滑滤波器，专门针对4D表示设计。通过非参数估计方法估计时间与焦距-深度比的联合密度函数，在推理阶段从该联合分布中采样以确定适当的平滑滤波器强度。滤波器强度根据局部运动信息自适应调整，可灵活集成到各种4D表示中。

Result: 在标准数据集上的评估表明，该方法在性能上优于现有最先进方法的混叠抑制效果。

Conclusion: 运动感知的3D平滑滤波器能够在不牺牲渲染质量的前提下有效缓解4D动态场景表示中的混叠伪影，且具有良好的通用性，可集成到不同的4D表示框架中。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis of dynamic scenes, crucial for AR/VR applications, remains a challenging problem. Recent methods adapt representations like 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) for dynamic scenes by incorporating time as the fourth dimension (4D representations). These 4D representations still suffer from aliasing artifacts, especially when generating novel views from divergent viewpoints (zoom-in/zoom-out operations). While using 3D smoothing filters like those proposed in Mip-Splatting might seem like a possible solution, they fail to account for local motion and also exhibit aliasing. To address this, we propose a motion-aware 3D smoothing filter specifically designed for 4D representations. Our approach adapts the filter strength based on local motion information, effectively mitigating aliasing without compromising rendering quality. This is achieved by estimating the joint density function of time and focal-to-depth ratio using a non-parametric estimation method. During inference, we sample from this joint distribution to determine the appropriate smoothing filter. This flexible strategy can be integrated with various 4D representations. Our evaluations on standard datasets demonstrate superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [7] [Action-Aligned Retrieval with Pairwise Multimodal Reranking for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23503)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thanh-Nhan Vo,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires distinguishing individuals based on fine-grained, context-dependent behaviors rather than mere appearance. Existing methods struggle to capture these context-conditioned actions, frequently relying on isolated skeletal geometry, discarding raw query details during reformulation, or utilizing absolute pointwise scoring for multimodal verification. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{ActPair}, a unified three-stage coarse-to-fine framework that combines action-aligned retrieval with pairwise multimodal reranking to bridge the pose-semantic gap. First, we fine-tune a vision-language model (VLM) with an action-aligned multi-task objective that encourages the representations to encode action-discriminative semantics. Second, we perform parallel late-fusion retrieval using the original query and a large language model (LLM)-generated context-grounded rewrite, retaining complementary details from both semantic views. Finally, we propose an efficient off-the-shelf reranking module that leverages a pivot-promote algorithm to perform direct pairwise visual comparisons, mitigating residual spatial and compositional ambiguities without the prohibitive inference costs of exhaustive evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves the best results among the compared methods on the Pedestrian Anomaly Behavior (PAB) public test and transfers effectively to an unseen, non-anomaly-specific dataset.

</details>


### [8] [Learning Sample-wise Rank-aware Interpolation Weights for Composed Visual Data Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22500)
*Boseung Jeong,Taegyu Park,Donghyeon Kwon,Hyunsouk Cho,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SRAIN框架，通过动态预测查询特定的线性插值权重实现组合视觉数据检索，在不使用多模态大语言模型的情况下达到与MLLM方法相当甚至更优的性能，同时大幅降低查询时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前组合视觉检索方法依赖多模态大语言模型进行参考视觉输入与文本修改的融合，虽然效果好但查询时延过高，限制了可扩展性。作者希望探索更轻量级的替代方案，即在嵌入空间中进行简单线性插值，但难点在于最优插值权重的定义与预测十分困难。

Method: SRAIN框架引入两项关键创新：(1) 训练阶段使用批次级排名感知的权重估计，衡量插值嵌入对负样本的判别力与对真实目标的接近程度；(2) 推理阶段使用紧凑的记忆库综合生成困难负样本，从而支持动态预测查询特定的插值权重。

Result: SRAIN在组合视频检索任务上取得最佳表现，在组合图像检索任务上与当前最先进方法持平，同时相比基于MLLM的方法大幅降低了查询时延。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视嵌入空间中简单线性插值的有效性，并引入动态权重预测机制，SRAIN在兼顾性能的同时解决了MLLM方法的时延瓶颈，为组合视觉检索提供了更可扩展的轻量化路径。

Abstract: At the heart of composed visual data retrieval is the fusion of a reference visual input and a textual modification into a single query. While current state-of-the-art methods utilize multimodal large language models for this fusion, their complexity introduces prohibitive querytime latency, limiting their scalability. We instead revisit the efficacy of simple linear interpolation within an embedding space, and introduce SRAIN, the first framework that dynamically predicts query-specific interpolation weights. The key challenge lies in the fact that the quality of an interpolation weight should be measured by the interpolated embedding's discriminability from negatives as well as its proximity to true targets; this makes collecting and predicting optimal weights intractable. We overcome this bottleneck through two key innovations: batch-wise rank-aware weight estimation during training, and a compact memory bank that synthesizes hard negatives during inference. SRAIN achieves the best in composed video retrieval and matches the current state of the art in composed image retrieval, all while substantially reducing querytime latency compared to MLLM-based alternatives.

</details>


### [9] [ORBIT++: Benchmarking SfM in the Wild with 360° Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22039)
*Sara Sabour,Linyi Jin,Richard Tucker,Amir Hertz,Marcus Brubaker,Saurabh Saxena,Junhwa Hur,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Deqing Sun,David J. Fleet,Richard Szeliski,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ORBIT，一个基于在线全景360°视频构建的相机位姿估计基准测试集，用于评估SfM方法在复杂真实场景（如困难相机运动或动态场景）中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SfM方法在处理涉及挑战性相机运动或动态场景的复杂视频时经常失败，且该领域缺乏针对此类困难场景的可靠地面真值基准，难以衡量实际进展或定位需要改进之处。

Method: 利用在线全景360°视频作为数据源，全景特性为追踪相机运动提供更丰富的视觉上下文（即使部分视野受模糊、运动或动态物体影响）；通过在全景视频中追踪相机运动后，裁剪并重投影选定部分生成透视视图片段，构建名为ORBIT的基准测试集。

Result: 实验表明COLMAP以及近期基于优化的和前馈式SfM方法在ORBIT基准上均难以准确估计相机位姿，验证了该基准的挑战性。

Conclusion: ORBIT为研究人员提供了一个有价值的测试平台，能够有意义地衡量在真正具有挑战性的真实世界SfM问题上的进展。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion (SfM) is a cornerstone of 3D perception, yet current methods often fail when applied to complex videos involving challenging camera motions or dynamic scenes. Compounding the problem, the field lacks reliable ground-truth benchmarks for such difficult scenarios, making it hard to gauge real-world progress or to pinpoint where improvements are most needed. To address this gap, we introduce a new benchmark for evaluating camera pose estimation. Our key insight is to leverage online panoramic 360° video as a source of data from which to construct challenging clips, while still enabling robust ground-truth trajectory recovery. The panoramic nature of these videos provides richer visual context for tracking camera motion, even when parts of the view are affected by blur, motion, or dynamic objects. After tracking camera motion across full 360° videos, we crop and reproject selected portions to generate perspective-view clips that serve as our benchmark, called ORBIT. Experiments show that COLMAP, as well as recent optimization-based and feed-forward SfM methods struggle to accurately estimate camera poses on our benchmark. Hence, ORBIT provides a valuable testbed where researchers can meaningfully measure progress on truly challenging, real-world SfM problems.

</details>


### [10] [The Plan, Not the Decoder: Diagnosing and Repairing Compositional Failure in Reasoning-Augmented Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21713)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究推理增强的文生图模型（如GoT-R1）中，当组合提示词生成失败时，问题究竟出在生成文本计划（planner）还是图像解码器（decoder）。研究发现解码器是忠实的执行者（94%的生成布局实现了计划中的关系），瓶颈在规划器。通过编辑文本计划（不重训练）即可修复图像：符号验证+重采样提升+5.0分，重写框几何提升+10.7分，替换整个计划提升+13.3分。研究还揭示规划器存在栅格顺序偏差和左右不对称偏差，并发布了评估协议、计划和1.2万张生成图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理增强的文生图模型在组合性提示词上经常失败，但此前无法确定失败来源——是模型生成的显式文本计划（对象名、属性、边界框）本身就错误，还是计划正确而解码器不忠实地执行计划。由于计划是机器可读的、可在解码前编辑，这两个因素得以分离，促使研究者系统性地验证两者的贡献。

Method: 提出"计划-解码"分离验证框架：1）用边界框交换实验验证测量工具（检测器得分比VQA分数更可靠，通过五名标注者验证）；2）在几何评分下测试解码器忠实度；3）用提及顺序控制实验隔离规划器的栅格顺序偏差；4）通过符号验证+重采样、最小就地修复、重写框几何、替换整个计划等四种计划编辑策略测试"只改计划不重训练"的效果；5）验证计划内部一致性（框-文本矛盾导致对象重复和身份融合）对模块化设计的影响。

Result: 1）交换边界框后检测器精度从0.75降至0.48（p<1e-3），而VQA指标反而上升，人类五评者81%同意检测器、57%同意VQA，故所有空间结果采用几何评分；2）解码器忠实度高：94%的生成布局实现了计划关系，对象-框绑定在计划重排序后保持稳定；3）规划器是瓶颈：对"left"的准确率98%但对语义相同的"right"仅54%，存在栅格顺序偏差；4）计划编辑提升显著：符号验证+重采样+5.0、就地修复+6.0、重写框几何+10.7、替换计划+13.3，增益与文风和计划似然无关但与几何有关；5）框-文本矛盾会导致对象重复和身份融合。

Conclusion: 模块化的"规划器-解码器"架构是可行的，前提是计划内部必须一致。解码器是忠实的执行者，瓶颈在于规划器的几何规划和关系推理能力。研究证明不经过重新训练、仅通过编辑计划就能显著修复生成图像，为文生图模型的调试和升级提供了新的、轻量级的路径。研究还揭示了规划器存在偏侧性与栅格顺序等系统性偏差，并公开了完整的评估协议和数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Reasoning-augmented text-to-image models such as GoT-R1 emit an explicit textual plan - object names, attributes, and bounding boxes - before generating image tokens. When such a model fails a compositional prompt, is the plan wrong, or is the plan right and the decoder unfaithful? Because the plan is machine-readable it can be edited before decoding, which makes the two separable. We first validate the ruler. Swapping the two bounding boxes inside the model's own chain demonstrably flips the generated layout: detector-based accuracy falls 0.75 -> 0.48 (p<1e-3), while a widely used VQA-based spatial metric rises. A five-rater human study agrees with the detector on 81% of items and with the VQA judge on 57%. All spatial results therefore use geometric scoring. Under sound measurement the decoder is a faithful executor: 94% of generated layouts realize the planned relation, and object-box binding survives reordering of the plan's object segments. The planner is the bottleneck. It writes wrong relations for phrasing-dependent reasons - 98% accuracy on "left" against 54% on "right" for semantically identical layouts, a raster-order bias we isolate with a mention-order control - and cluttered geometry that the decoder faithfully reproduces. Editing the plan therefore fixes the image without retraining: symbolic verification with resampling gives +5.0 points (p<1e-3), minimal in-place repair +6.0 (p=.02), rewriting only box geometry +10.7 (p<1e-4), and replacing the plan outright +13.3 (p=1e-4). Gains are indifferent to the plan's prose style and to its likelihood under the planner, but not to its geometry. Modular planner-decoder designs are therefore viable, provided the plan is internally consistent: box-text contradictions induce object duplication and identity fusion. We release the plan-fidelity evaluation protocol, all plans, and 12k generated images.

</details>


### [11] [A Scalable Vector Graphics Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21893)
*Leonardo Zini,Elia Frigieri,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLS，一个基于Transformer的SVG路径自编码器，通过BPE分词构建统一的紧凑稠密潜在空间，支持高保真重建、相似度搜索、组合操作等下游任务，相比token方法FLOPs降低150倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG是分辨率无关的视觉内容基础媒介，但深度学习领域缺乏类似VAE之于栅格图像那样的连续、稠密、可逆的矢量表示潜在空间。现有的矢量表示方法缺少这一基础构建模块。

Method: SLS采用Transformer自编码器架构，将SVG命令、坐标数据和视觉属性统一编码为基于BPE的分词词汇表，学习固定大小的潜在表示，该表示同时捕获路径的结构和外观信息，并能解码为有效的、风格一致的SVG路径。嵌入空间位于单位超球面上，支持高效的向量空间操作。

Result: SLS的嵌入空间具有鲁棒性、可逆性和结构性的特点。在跨多种下游任务中展现出良好的泛化能力，相比基于token的方法FLOPs降低了150倍以上，验证了其作为矢量图形通用潜在基础的可行性。

Conclusion: SLS建立了矢量图形研究中的通用潜在空间基础，实现了类似VAE对栅格图像那样简单、高效的矢量表示操作，为SVG的相似度搜索、组合和条件生成等应用提供了全新范式。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics are a fundamental medium for resolution-independent visual content, yet the deep learning community lacks a continuous, dense, and invertible latent space for vector representations, the kind of foundational building block that Variational Autoencoders and their descendants have long provided for raster images. We introduce SLS (SVG Latent Space), a Transformer-based autoencoder that learns compact dense representations of individual SVG paths, the atomic visual elements from which any SVG image can be composed. By modeling SVG commands, coordinate data, and visual properties within a unified BPE-based token vocabulary, SLS learns fixed-size latent representations that jointly capture structure and appearance, and can be decoded back into valid, style-consistent SVG paths with high fidelity. The resulting embedding space is robust, invertible, and structured: embeddings lie on a unit hypersphere, enabling efficient similarity search, composition, and downstream conditioning through simple vector-space operations. Finally, we demonstrate that SLS generalizes across diverse tasks reducing their FLOPs by over 150 times compared to token-based approaches, and establishing a general-purpose latent foundation for vector graphics research.

</details>


### [12] [EXPL-FR: Explaining Face Recognition Models via Vision-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21486)
*Guray Ozgur,Mustafa Efe Tamyapar,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EXPL-FR是一个用于深度人脸识别模型的可解释性框架，通过在FR模型的嵌入空间内对齐视觉语言模型（VLM）的图像编码器，将自然语言属性提示转换为FR空间的锚点，从而在不访问架构的前提下提供身份级、逐图像和差分解释，并支持完全无标签的基于提示的审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度人脸识别模型虽然精度接近饱和，但其决策过程不透明，从业者无法询问相似度分数依赖了哪些语义属性。现有方法缺乏在FR模型自身嵌入空间内提供可解释性的方案。

Method: 使用轻量级适配器将冻结的VLM图像编码器与冻结的FR空间对齐，仅用面部图像训练、不使用文本。由于VLM编码器共享同一空间，同一适配器也适用于文本编码器，从而将978个属性提示（22个类别，可扩展）转化为FR空间锚点。通过人脸验证协议衡量迁移是否有效，并使用消融实验隔离适配器贡献。提出无标签的可检测性度量，比较各概念在FR空间与VLM空间中的可分性，选取100个最可检测的概念构成模型的"可读语义签名"。

Result: 覆盖四种FR骨干网络和两种VLM编码器。属性级审计在三种监督设置下（人工标签、VLM伪标签、完全提示驱动）进行基准测试。无标签的提示驱动审计能够在RFW数据集上按实测各族裔错误率对四种FR模型进行排序，并能按真实验证代价对受控属性变化进行排序。

Conclusion: 并非所有概念都能在FR空间中存活，因为FR模型通过丢弃身份验证所需的无关因素来获得不变性。100个最可检测概念构成的语义签名在区分身份方面的表现优于完整词汇表，且EXPL-FR无需架构访问即可工作，支持多种解释粒度和完全无标签的审计。

Abstract: Deep face recognition (FR) models reach near-saturated accuracy but remain opaque: a practitioner cannot ask which semantic attributes a similarity score relied upon. EXPL-FR answers this inside the FR model's own embedding space. A lightweight adapter aligns a vision-language model's (VLM) image encoder with the frozen FR space, trained on face images alone and never on text. Because the VLM's encoders share one space, the same adapter applies to the text encoder, turning 978 attribute prompts in 22 categories, also extendable, into FR-space anchors at no extra cost. We do not assume this transfer works: a face-verification protocol measures it, and an ablation changing only the adapter isolates its contribution. Not every concept survives, because an FR model earns its invariances by discarding the factors it must verify identities across. A label-free detectability measure compares each concept's separability in FR space against the VLM space, and the 100 most detectable form the model's readable semantic signature, which separates identities better than the full vocabulary. We cover four FR backbones and two VLM encoders, EXPL-FR needs no architecture access, and supports identity-level, per-image, and differential explanations. We benchmark attribute-level auditing under three supervision settings, human labels (current practice), VLM pseudo-labels, and our fully prompt-driven audit, against real verification behavior. With no labels, the prompt-driven audit ranks four FR models by their measured per-ethnicity RFW errors and ranks controlled attribute changes by their true verification cost.

</details>


### [13] [How Merge-Tolerant Are Vision Transformers for Wheat Phenotyping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23142)
*Simon Ravé,Pejman Rasti,David Rousseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了训练无需的token合并方法（ToMe和Mutual Pair Merging）在基于视觉的麦田表型分析任务（生长阶段分类、麦穗检测、麦粒器官分割）中的性能，涵盖任务质量、吞吐量、token数量和峰值GPU内存，并在Raspberry Pi 5上进行了边缘推理测量。结果表明分类对token合并高度容忍，而检测和分割受限于重复实例、细薄器官、密集边界、重建和运行时开销；优化后的注意力后端可能抵消看似可观的加速，部署价值须在实际运行环境中评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的小麦表型分析需要在部署约束下进行重复测量，涵盖生长阶段识别、麦穗计数和器官分割等任务。普通Vision Transformer（ViT）提供了统一架构，但二次复杂度注意力限制了高吞吐量和边缘推理。训练无需的token合并方法因可直接插入已训练模型而无需重训练，具备吸引力，但缺乏系统性的多任务基准评估。

Method: 对ToMe（Token Merging）和Mutual Pair Merging两种训练无需token合并方法，在生长阶段分类、麦穗检测和小麦器官分割三个任务上进行系统基准测试，测量任务质量、吞吐量、token数量和峰值GPU内存，并额外在Raspberry Pi 5上进行边缘设备测量。

Result: 基准测试揭示了清晰的层级：分类任务对token合并高度容忍，而检测和分割任务受重复实例、细薄器官、密集边界及重建和运行时开销的限制。此外，优化后的注意力后端可消除在token减少上看似获得的加速效果。

Conclusion: 部署价值必须基于目标运行环境进行性能剖析，而不能仅从token数量推断得出。

Abstract: Vision-based wheat phenotyping requires repeated measurements under deployment constraints, from growth-stage recognition to wheat-head counting and organ segmentation. Plain Vision Transformers (ViTs) provide a common architecture for these tasks, but quadratic attention limits high-throughput and edge inference. Training-free token merging is attractive because it can be inserted into trained models without retraining. We provide a systematic benchmark of ToMe and Mutual Pair Merging across growth-stage classification, wheat-head detection, and wheat-organ segmentation, measuring task quality, throughput, token count, and peak GPU memory, with additional Raspberry Pi 5 measurements. The benchmark reveals a clear hierarchy: classification is highly merge-tolerant, while detection and segmentation are constrained by repeated instances, thin organs, dense boundaries, reconstruction, and runtime overhead. Optimized attention backends can erase apparent speedups, so deployment value must be profiled on the target runtime rather than inferred from token count.

</details>


### [14] [SpatialDiff: 3D-Aware Object Movement via Implicit Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21776)
*Zheng Liu,Zijian He,Huiguo He,Weizhi Zhong,Yejun Tang,Huan Yang,Kun Gai,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialDiff方法，通过隐式3D空间建模和全局空间监督，实现复杂场景中物体的精确、一致性空间移动编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法在处理复杂场景中的空间移动（如物体跨越不同深度层或被部分遮挡）时表现不佳。大多方法仅依赖2D数据集的先验信息，强调平面特征而缺乏空间结构支持；即使引入显式位置信息的方法也无法捕获真实的3D空间关系，限制了复杂场景中物体移动的准确性。

Method: 提出SpatialDiff方法，包含两大核心创新：(1) 隐式3D空间建模——引入3D先验知识，使模型在内部构建三维空间结构的全面理解；(2) 全局空间监督——约束潜在空间特征，使模型能够感知编辑操作引起的物体空间位置变化。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在复杂场景中的空间移动准确性和保真度方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: SpatialDiff通过隐式3D建模与全局空间监督相结合，有效解决了现有方法在复杂场景中物体空间移动的局限，为图像编辑提供了更精确的3D空间感知能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in image editing allow impressive manipulation of objects, existing methods still struggle to handle spatial movement in complex scenes, such as objects span different depth layers or are partially occluded. Most image editing methods focus solely on prior information from 2D datasets, emphasizing planar features while lacking support for spatial structures. Even approaches that incorporate explicit positional information fail to capture true 3D spatial relationships, thus limiting accurate object movement in complex scenes. In this paper, we present SpatialDiff, a method that effectively captures 3D spatial structures, enabling precise and consistent object movements in complex scenes. Our core innovations are twofold: (1) Implicit 3D Spatial Modeling, which introduces 3D prior knowledge and enables the model to internally build a comprehensive understanding of the three-dimensional spatial structure; and (2) Global Spatial Supervision, which constrains the latent spatial features to enable the model to perceive changes in object spatial positions caused by editing operations. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves the accuracy and fidelity of spatial movement in complex scenes.

</details>


### [15] [LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Unified Removal of Raindrops and Reflections: Methods and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22723)
*Zewei He,Xi Tong,Yu Chen,Xingyu Liu,Xin Li,Zepeng Wang,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou,Minmin Yi,Chuanrui Zhang,Liwen Zhang,Yeongjin Jeong,Hyunjin Cho,Jiwon Lee,Minsang Kim,Jae Woong Soh,Jin-Hui Jiang,Rong-Lin Jian,Chih-Chung Hsu,Youngjin Oh,Junhyeong Kwon,Junyoung Park,Jae Hyun Park,Sung Ju Lee,Nam Ik Cho,Vishwajeet Shukla,Himanshu Baurai,Zhiqi Zhang,Kui Jiang,Zhaocheng Yu,Runzhe Li,Dawei Fan,Hao Li,Zhanshuo Zhang,Fan Ji,Jiangmeng Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Shangquan Sun,Anh-Kiet Duong,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza,Ruibo Zhang,Dexiang Hong,Xinyan Liu,Shengeng Tang,Weidong Chen,Tzu-Hsuan Weng,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文全面评述了首届雨滴与反射联合去除挑战赛（First Challenge on Unified Removal of Raindrops and Reflections），该比赛旨在解决自动驾驶中雨天天候下雨滴-反射复合退化问题，吸引了149名注册参与者，收到12份有效的最终提交方案及其说明书。所有方法均在真实的RDRF（RainDrop and ReFlection）数据集上开发和评估，报告详细分析了提交的方法和结果，突出了有效方法并为未来研究提供了有价值的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在雨天天候下面临雨滴和反射的复合图像退化问题，这是实际场景中频繁遇到但尚未被充分解决的难题，需要组织挑战赛来推动该领域的研究进展。

Method: 组织首届雨滴与反射联合去除挑战赛，在自建的真实RDRF数据集上对参赛方法进行开发和评估，对12份有效提交的方案及其结果进行系统化分析和比较。

Result: 比赛共吸引149名注册参与者并收到12份有效的最终提交方案及相应说明书，参赛方法显著推进了雨滴与反射联合去除技术的发展，报告对有效方法进行了详细分析。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛成功推动了雨滴与反射统一去除领域的发展，报告为未来的相关研究提供了有效的技术路线和深入见解。

Abstract: This workshop paper comprehensively reviews the First Challenge on Unified Removal of Raindrops and Reflections. The challenge aims to address a frequently encountered practical problem in the field of autonomous driving, i.e., raindrop-reflection composite degradation on rainy days. This competition attracted 149 registered participants and received 12 valid final submissions with corresponding fact sheets, significantly contributing to the progress of unified removal of raindrops and reflections. All the methods are developed and evaluated on our real-shot RainDrop and ReFlection (RDRF) dataset. A detailed analysis of the submitted methods and corresponding results is provided in this report, which highlights effective approaches and provides interesting insights for future research.

</details>


### [16] [HeatTok: Enhancing Remote Sensing Image Understanding via Thermodiffusion-based Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22485)
*Yingying Yan,Jiaqi Tang,Wei Wei,Qianzhou Wang,Jinjian Wu,Botong Geng,Jianmin Chen,Yuyang Xia,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeatTok是一种面向遥感图像的语义感知Tokenizer，通过热扩散聚合机制自适应合并同质区域生成与地物对象对齐的不规则语义Token，并设计G-MRoPE位置编码注入中心、尺度和方向信息，在VRSBench和EarthVQA上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM的视觉Tokenizer主要基于patch划分，由于遥感地物轮廓不规则，导致严重的语义混杂和对象碎片化问题。已有的自适应方法难以提取精确的对象级Token，且缺乏针对不规则区域的几何位置编码。

Method: 1) HeatTok：受热传导物理原理启发，通过热扩散聚合机制自适应合并相邻同质区域，生成语义独立、对象对齐的不规则Token；2) G-MRoPE (Gaussian Multimodal Rotary Positional Embedding)：利用2D高斯模型刻画Token空间分布，显式注入中心、尺度和方向等几何线索。

Result: 在VRSBench和EarthVQA数据集上的广泛实验表明，HeatTok能有效保持对象级语义完整性，并在合理的Token预算下取得最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: HeatTok通过热扩散驱动的语义感知Tokenization和G-MRoPE几何位置编码，有效解决了遥感图像中patch划分导致的语义混杂与对象碎片化问题，为MLLMs提供了对象级对齐的高质量视觉Token。

Abstract: Current visual tokenizers in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on patch-based partitioning, which causes severe semantic mixture and object fragmentation in remote sensing imagery due to the irregular contours of geo-objects. Moreover, existing adaptive methods struggle to extract precise object-level tokens and lack dedicated geometric positional encodings for irregular regions. In this paper, we propose HeatTok, a semantic-aware tokenizer driven by thermodiffusion aggregation. Inspired by the physical principles of heat conduction, HeatTok adaptively merges adjacent homogeneous regions to generate semantically independent, object-aligned irregular tokens. To enable MLLMs to perceive these irregular shapes, we design the Gaussian Multimodal Rotary Positional Embedding (G-MRoPE), which models token spatial distributions via 2D Gaussians and explicitly injects center, scale, and orientation cues. Extensive evaluations on the VRSBench and EarthVQA datasets demonstrate that HeatTok effectively preserves object-level semantic integrity and achieves state-of-the-art performance under a reasonable token budget. The code is available: https://github.com/YingyingYan1/HeatTok.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Visual Similarity: Entity-Aligned Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21450)
*Hangrui Xu,Zhengxian Wu,Yunyao Yu,Zhuohong Chen,Rui Cong,Xiangwen Deng,Zhifang Liu,Peng Jiao,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KBMR，首个面向知识型视觉问答（KB-VQA）的基于MLLM的嵌入检索器。它利用MLLM的强自回归能力将图像映射到更保语义的概念空间中，并引入MLLM语义判别器生成连续的实体一致性权重，指导连续语义蒸馏目标，从而解决Wikipedia规模检索中的噪声监督问题。实验表明，KBMR相比CLIP基线在检索Recall@1上提升高达14.7%，端到端VQA准确率提升9.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA检索管线主要采用CLIP风格的双编码器，其倾向于表面视觉相似性而非实体级语义对齐。当语义相同的概念呈现较大视觉差异，或不同实体外观相似时，这种范式经常失效，无法准确支持长尾实体的外部知识检索。

Method: 提出KBMR框架：(1) 首个基于MLLM的嵌入检索器，利用MLLM的强自回归能力将图像映射到更好保留概念身份的语义空间；(2) 提出MLLM语义判别器，生称连续的实体一致性权重；(3) 提出连续语义蒸馏目标，利用这些权重实现有效的难负样本采样和超越刚性二值标签的软监督。

Result: 大量实验表明KBMR显著优于CLIP基线，在检索Recall@1上最多提升14.7%，端到端VQA准确率提升9.4%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: KBMR验证了MLLM作为嵌入检索器在KB-VQA中的有效性，通过连续语义蒸馏克服了噪声监督问题，为KB-VQA的知识检索提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) relies on retrieving external information to answer queries involving long-tail entities. However, existing retrieval pipelines predominantly employ CLIP-style dual encoders, which prioritize surface-level visual similarity over entity-level semantic alignment. This paradigm often fails when semantically identical concepts exhibit large visual variations or when distinct entities appear visually similar. To address this, we propose KBMR, the first MLLM-based embedding retriever tailored for KB-VQA. Leveraging the robust autoregressive capabilities of MLLMs, KBMR maps images into a semantic space that better preserves concept identity. To tackle the challenge of noisy supervision in Wikipedia-scale retrieval, we introduce an MLLM-based semantic discriminator that generates continuous entity-consistency weights. These weights guide a novel continuous semantic distillation objective, enabling effective hard negative sampling and soft supervision beyond rigid binary labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that KBMR significantly outperforms CLIP baselines, yielding up to a 14.7% improvement in retrieval Recall@1 and a 9.4% gain in end-to-end VQA accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/realHarryX/KBMR.

</details>


### [18] [ENCORE: Entropy-Guided Cropping and Attention Regularization for Robust Vision--Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22996)
*Yuanhao Sun,Huawei Ji,Jiaxin Ding,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ENCORE框架，利用交叉注意力的早期层图像-文本熵来指导图像裁剪和训练正则化，仅微调0.14%参数即在10个VQA基准上平均提升1.43%准确率，达到2B参数VLMs中的SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级VLM的transformer视觉编码器将图像分割为固定分辨率子图像，破坏了物体完整性；而现有方法仅关注视觉模态，无法动态保留与提示相关区域的完整性，限制了性能。作者观察到早期层交叉注意力的图像-文本熵与答案定位质量和任务准确率强相关，因此希望利用这一发现来提升VLM性能。

Method: 提出ENTROPY-guided框架ENCORE，包含两个组件：(1) 推理时的熵基裁剪策略(ECS)，评估少量候选裁剪区域并选择熵最小的区域，保留与提示相关的连续区域；(2) 训练时的熵正则化训练(ERT)，在下一个token预测中增加熵项，强化对关键视觉token的注意力并降低不相关token权重。

Result: 在10个VQA基准上，ENCORE仅微调0.14%的参数，实现了平均1.43%的准确率提升，并在近期2B参数VLMs中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过熵引导的裁剪和训练正则化，ENCORE能够动态保留与提示相关的图像区域完整性，以极少的参数微调代价显著提升轻量级VLM在VQA任务上的性能，验证了交叉注意力熵作为指导信号的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on diverse vision-language tasks, but transformer-based visual encoders split images into fixed-resolution sub-images, compromising object integrity in lightweight VLMs. Existing methods only focus on the visual modality and fail to dynamically preserve the integrity of prompt-relevant regions, limiting performance. In this work, we observe that the early-layer image-text entropy of cross-modal attention strongly correlates with answer grounding quality and task accuracy. Building on this finding, we propose \textbf{ENCORE}, an entropy-guided framework with two components: At inference, an \textbf{Entropy-based Cropping Strategy} (ECS) evaluates a small set of candidate crops and selects the one with minimal entropy, preserving contiguous regions relevant to the prompt. At training, \textbf{Entropy Regularization Training} (ERT) augments next-token prediction with an entropy term that sharpens attention on key visual tokens while down-weighting irrelevant ones. Experiments on ten VQA benchmarks show that ENCORE, fine-tuning only 0.14\% of parameters, achieves an average 1.43\% accuracy gain and state-of-the-art performance among recent 2B-parameter VLMs. Our code is released in https://github.com/baokou-fw2/ENCORE.

</details>


### [19] [Pre-Decoding Acoustic Triage for Budgeted Vision-Language Captioning of Untrimmed Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22359)
*Masoud Jalayer,Changyi Li,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"音频优先分诊"(audio-first triage)策略，在视频解码前仅用音频信号选择需要VLM调用的窗口，从而大幅降低长时间第一视角视频分析的推理成本。关键创新在于训练目标：选择器不是逐帧检测声音事件，而是针对每个动作触发一次，使动作覆盖率提升4.0-10.8个百分点。在EPIC-KITCHENS-100和Ego4D上，该方法用不到一半的调用次数削减9-20%的VLM调用，且优于均匀采样和两种视觉关键帧选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间第一视角（第一人称/穿戴式）视频自动分析在物流、建筑和制造等场景中日益重要，但当前流程使用VLM处理短窗口的调用成本过高，与预算约束相矛盾。已有的分诊策略要么均匀采样、要么依赖视觉特征排序，后者需要视频解码，而视频解码正是预算约束想要避免的开销。

Method: 提出音频优先分诊策略：在解码任何视频帧之前，仅使用最轻量的音频模态对窗口进行评分。训练目标不是逐帧声音事件检测，而是让选择器"每个动作触发一次"（trigger once per action），使用冻结的AudioSet预训练特征，无需领域特定的声音事件标签，并与token压缩或量化天然兼容。

Result: 在所有评估的调用率下，动作覆盖率提升4.0-10.8个百分点。在EPIC-KITCHENS-100上用不到一半的调用即可在匹配覆盖率下削减9-20%的VLM调用；在Ego4D的247个片段上超越均匀采样（中程范围）；性能优于两种最近的视觉关键帧选择器。

Conclusion: 音频优先分诊证明了利用最轻量模态在选择前先行过滤的有效性，其"每动作触发一次"的目标设计比传统声音事件检测更能匹配动作覆盖需求，为预算受限的长时间视频分析提供了低成本、可扩展且不依赖视频解码的实用方案。

Abstract: Automatically analyzing hours-long egocentric video is increasingly essential for progress monitoring, quality control, and safety in logistics, construction, and manufacturing. Yet current pipelines that process short, fixed-size windows with a vision-language model (VLM) are prohibitively expensive because cost scales with the number of model calls. To reduce this cost, prior work proposes triage policies to select which windows merit a VLM invocation. However, these policies either sample uniformly or rank windows using visual features, which ironically requires the video decoding that the budget constraints are meant to avoid. We propose audio-first triage: select windows using the lightest modality, scored before any video frame is decoded, so the approach composes naturally with token compression or quantization. The novelty lies in the objective, not the representation: rather than a per-frame sound-event detector, we train the selector to trigger once per action. This objective shift improves action coverage by 4.0-10.8 percentage points across all evaluated call rates, using frozen AudioSet-pretrained features without domain-specific sound-event labels. Using fewer than half of the available calls, the triage cuts 9-20% of VLM calls at matched coverage on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (EK-100), surpasses uniform sampling through the mid-range on Ego4D over 247 clips, and outperforms two recent visual keyframe selectors. Code, the reference implementation and every results file this manuscript reads are at https://github.com/masjalayer/PreDecoding-AcousticTriage.

</details>


### [20] [SketchFlow: Zero-Shot Vector Sketch Generation via GMM Prior Flow in CLIP Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21659)
*Jin Zhou,Hongliang Yang,Pengfei Xu,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SketchFlow是一个基于最优传输理论和流匹配的新型生成框架，用于生成具有人类绘画风格的矢量笔画。它利用预训练CLIP模型绕过文本标注，通过在高斯混合模型先验和目标草图特征分布之间学习确定性向量场，再配合混合扩散解码器生成快速高保真的笔画轨迹，在视觉质量、人类绘画风格遵循度和零样本生成方面均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量草图是简洁直观的抽象表达媒介，但生成具有人类风格的高质量矢量笔画仍面临挑战，主要因为：1) 细粒度、高质量文本到草图配对数据严重匮乏；2) 现有文本条件生成方法依赖不稳定且耗时的优化过程；3) 难以在零样本条件下泛化到未见类别。

Method: 1) 利用预训练CLIP模型绕过人工图像级文本标注，直接在CLIP潜在空间中将跨模态对齐建模为连续映射问题；2) 向离散类别嵌入注入噪声构造连续高斯混合模型先验，以弥合离散文本概念与连续草图特征之间的模态差距；3) 采用最优传输条件流匹配模型学习从GMM先验到目标草图特征分布的确定性向量场；4) 设计融合1D U-Net和Transformer的混合扩散解码器，将特征解码为快速高保真的笔画轨迹。

Result: 大量实验表明SketchFlow在视觉质量和人类自然绘画风格遵循度上显著优于现有基线。该几何保持框架能够对超出QuickDraw训练词汇表的提示（包括未见概念标签和语义修饰符）实现局部零样本合成，并支持不同概念之间的平滑连续语义插值。

Conclusion: SketchFlow通过最优传输流匹配和CLIP潜在空间中的连续建模，有效解决了草图生成中配对数据稀缺和零样本泛化的问题，为高质量矢量草图生成提供了一种无需图像级文本标注的可行方案。

Abstract: Vector sketches remain one of the most concise and immediate mediums for abstract human expression. However, generating high-quality vector strokes that exhibit human-like drawing styles remains an open challenge due to the severe scarcity of fine-grained, high-quality text-to-sketch paired data. Existing text-conditioned generation methods often rely on unstable, time-consuming optimization or struggle to generalize to unseen categories in a zero-shot manner. To address these limitations, we present SketchFlow, a novel generative framework rooted in Optimal Transport (OT) theory and flow matching. By leveraging pre-trained CLIP models to bypass labor-intensive image-level text annotations, we formulate cross-modal alignment as a continuous mapping problem directly within the CLIP latent space. To bridge the inevitable modality gap between discrete text concepts and continuous sketch features, we first inject noise into discrete category embeddings to construct a continuous Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) prior. We then utilize an Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching (OT-CFM) model to learn a deterministic vector field mapping from this continuous GMM prior to the target sketch feature distribution. Finally, a Hybrid Diffusion Decoder, fusing 1D U-Net and Transformer architectures, is designed to decode these features into fast and high-fidelity stroke trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SketchFlow substantially outperforms existing baselines in visual quality and adherence to natural human drawing styles. Furthermore, our geometry-preserving framework demonstrates promising local zero-shot synthesis for prompts beyond the QuickDraw training vocabulary, including unseen concept labels and semantic modifiers, while enabling smooth, continuous semantic interpolation between distinct concepts. Source code is available at: https://github.com/QiuHong-1202/SketchFlow.

</details>


### [21] [Spatiotemporally Decoupled Autoregressive Diffusion Model for Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23279)
*Chengqun Yang,Liang Xu,Yanping Li,Fulong Liu,Jingnan Gao,Weili Zeng,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一时空解耦框架DeMoDiff，通过空间-时序VAE对每个身体关节进行编码而非压缩到单一潜空间，并结合含空间-时序掩码与注意力机制的自回归扩散生成器，在HumanML3D和KIT-ML数据集上实现SOTA的重建性能和运动生成效果，同时具备强时空编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动运动合成方法存在两类核心缺陷：(1) 表征层面，VQ类方法存在固有信息损失，损害生成质量、多样性与泛化性；连续表征处理整体全身运动缺乏部位级灵活性；(2) 架构层面，扩散与自回归扩散模型对单个身体部位的细粒度控制能力有限。因此需要同时改进表征与生成架构以兼顾性能与可控性。

Method: 提出DeMoDiff框架：1) 提出空间-时序VAE（Spatial-Temporal VAE），对每个身体关节分别编码而非将全身运动压缩到单一潜空间，增强表征提取能力并提供部位级可控性；2) 在自回归扩散生成器中融入空间-时序掩码与注意力机制，同时实现生成能力与可编辑性；3) 在时间和空间两个维度进行解耦设计，构建统一的生成框架。

Result: 在HumanML3D和KIT-ML基准数据集上取得SOTA的重建性能和运动生成效果；同时展现出强大的时间和空间编辑能力。

Conclusion: DeMoDiff通过联合重新设计运动表征与生成架构，有效解决了VQ方法信息损失与连续表征缺乏部位灵活性的问题，在生成质量与细粒度可控性之间取得平衡，验证了时空解耦框架在文本驱动运动合成及编辑任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Text-driven human motion synthesis has made substantial development with two core modules of motion representation and generative architecture. For representation, Vector Quantization (VQ)-based methods compress motion data into discrete tokens while latent-based models operate directly in continuous space. However, both of these representations exhibit significant limitations. VQ-based methods suffer from inherent information loss, which compromises the quality, diversity, and generalization of generated motions, while continuous representation on holistic whole-body motion hinders part-level flexibility. For architecture, diffusion and autoregressive diffusion models have demonstrated their superiority, yet the fine-grained controllability over individual body parts is also limited. Thus, we propose a unified spatiotemporally decoupled framework named DeMoDiff, which jointly redesigns representation and architecture. To enhance representation extraction capabilities and offer greater part-level controllability, we present a spatial-temporal VAE that encodes each body joint rather than compressing the whole-body motion into a single latent space. Then, we incorporate spatial-temporal masking and attention mechanisms into an autoregressive diffusion generator, achieving both generative capability and controllable editability. Extensive experiments on the HumanML3D and KIT-ML datasets demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction performance and compelling motion generation results. Moreover, our framework demonstrates strong temporal and spatial editing capabilities, further validating its effectiveness. Our project page: https://rex0191.github.io/DeMoDiff/

</details>


### [22] [BIMScript: Material-Aware Structured Scene Programs for BIM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21447)
*Prakash Kondibhau Naikade,Thomas B. Moeslund,Andreas Møgelmose*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BIMScript通过单一语法扩展结构化语言模型，同时解决场景理解的三个核心问题：内容（what）、速度（how fast）和位置（where）。它引入材料与条件属性（由VLM构建的100k合成场景材料护照语料库监督），采用CUDA-graph解码器将解码速度提升3.4倍，并通过几何吸附和混合离散-连续解码头解决5cm网格粒度误差，最终通过Revit插件实现端到端的BIM直接导入验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化语言模型（如SceneScript）将场景重建为参数化命令的短程序，具有天然可编辑和语义明确的特点。作者希望将其应用于最具吸引力的场景——将现有建筑自动导入BIM工具。但要实现这一目标，必须回答三个问题：场景由什么构成（内容）、生成速度有多快、每个元素的确切位置在哪里。这三个问题分别对应语义完整性、推理效率和空间精度三大挑战。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 扩展布局语言，为每个元素添加材料和条件属性，通过构建100k合成场景（190万伪标记元素）的VLM材料护照语料库进行监督，并使用提升特征点编码器将图像外观路由到材料标记；(2) 识别出自回归解码的瓶颈在于内核启动和主机同步开销而非计算量，提出输出精确的CUDA-graph解码器（1.9 vs 6.4 ms/步，提速3.4倍）以及利用确定性实体模式的语法并行、容差验证的草稿-验证方案；(3) 针对模型5cm标记网格粒度，提出无需训练的几何吸附和混合离散-连续解码头（回归子网格偏移），并量化各自对残差的恢复程度。

Result: CUDA-graph解码器将每步解码时间从6.4ms降至1.9ms，获得3.4倍加速；材料护照语料库提供了190万伪标记元素用于材料属性监督；通过几何吸附和子网格偏移回归有效减少了位置误差；端到端验证中，每个命令与原生Revit对象一一对应，通过可用的加载项和IFC4导出在BIM创作工具中验证了直接导入流程。

Conclusion: BIMScript证明了一个统一语法可以同时解决BIM自动导入中的内容、速度和精度三个问题。其语言形式不仅支持直接导入BIM创作工具，还设计用于支持LLM驱动的、面向可持续性的建筑资产推理，为结构化语言模型在BIM领域的实际部署提供了完整的范式。

Abstract: Structured-language models such as SceneScript reconstruct a scene as a short program of parametric commands, an inherently editable and semantically explicit representation. We ask three questions that stand between such models and their most compelling application, automated ingestion of existing buildings into BIM tools, studied here on synthetic scans: \emph{what} is the scene made of, \emph{how fast} can it be produced, and \emph{exactly where} is each element. BIMScript answers all three within one grammar. First, we extend the layout language with per-element \emph{material} and \emph{condition} attributes, supervised by a vision-language-model material-passport corpus we build over 100k synthetic scenes (1.9M pseudo-labeled elements), and route image appearance to the material tokens through a lifted-feature point encoder. Second, we show that autoregressive decoding of these programs is dominated not by compute but by kernel-launch and host-synchronization overhead, and remove it with an output-exact CUDA-graph decoder (1.9 vs 6.4\,ms/step, $3.4\times$) plus a grammar-parallel, tolerance-verified draft-and-verify scheme that exploits the deterministic entity schema. Third, we address the model's 5cm token-grid granularity with training-free geometric snapping and a hybrid discrete--continuous decoder head that regresses a sub-bin offset, and measure how much of the residual error each recovers. Because each command maps one-to-one onto a native Revit object, we validate direct ingestion into a BIM authoring tool end to end with a working add-in and its IFC4 export, and the same program's language form is designed to support LLM-driven, sustainability-aware reasoning over the built asset.

</details>


### [23] [FixAnything: 3D-Consistent Rendering Refinement via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23549)
*Khiem Vuong,Deva Ramanan,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FixAnything是一个统一的模型，通过复用预训练的视频生成模型，以轻量级微调和最小修改的方式修复多种3D表示（如3DGS、NeRF、网格、点云）中的渲染伪影。核心思想是将清理任务视为视频到视频的翻译，引入二进制掩码锚定高质量输入区域，并使用相机位姿精度作为DPO奖励信号来保证3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散生成先验的渲染伪影修复方法针对单一3D表示定制，需要自定义架构和大量重训练，缺乏跨表示的通用性。作者希望开发一个统一的模型，能够修复多种3D表示中的渲染伪影。

Method: 1) 复用预训练视频生成模型，仅需最小修改和轻量级微调；2) 引入二进制掩码标记干净像素，使模型锚定高质量输入并精炼其余部分；3) 使用结构从运动（SfM）恢复的相机位姿精度作为直接偏好优化（DPO）的奖励信号，促进3D一致性输出。

Result: 在四种不同的3D表示上，FixAnything一致地提升了渲染质量，且仅需轻量级微调，证明单一通用视频先验可替代多个专用精炼管线。

Conclusion: 统一通用视频先验可以替代多种专用伪影修复管线，且框架简单，能够即插即用地适配未来更强的视频模型，无需架构重新设计。

Abstract: Rendering views using 3D scene representations such as Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), meshes, or even point clouds produces artifacts when input views are sparse or target views lie far from the input. Recent work mitigates these artifacts using diffusion-based generative priors, but is specialized to individual representations and require custom architectures or extensive retraining. We present FixAnything, a single model for fixing a wide range of rendering artifacts. It does so by repurposing a pretrained video generative model, leveraging its implicit multi-view priors with only minimal modification and lightweight finetuning. Our key insight is that even noisily-rendered sequences preserve camera motion and coarse scene structure, allowing cleanup to be formulated as video-to-video translation. To control what scene structure should be preserved, we introduce a binary mask denoting the clean pixels, enabling the model to anchor its output to high-quality inputs (e.g. training views) while refining the rest. To encourage FixAnything to produce 3D-consistent renderings that support downstream reconstruction, we use camera pose accuracy (recovered via structure-from-motion) as a reward signal for direct preference optimization (DPO). Across four distinct 3D representations, FixAnything consistently improves rendering quality with lightweight finetuning, demonstrating that a single generalist video prior can replace multiple specialist refinement pipelines. The simplicity of the framework enables immediate adoption of stronger future video models without architectural redesign.

</details>


### [24] [Training-Free Pseudo-Fusion for Composed Image Retrieval with Diffusion Models and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23102)
*Fan Xu,Luis A. Leiva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PeFuse，一个无需训练的零样本组合图像检索（CIR）框架，利用预训练的扩散模型和多模态大语言模型，通过生成式转换将CIR任务重构为四个单模态检索问题，在标准基准上达到与最先进方法竞争或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CIR方法依赖多模态融合模块将视觉和文本特征组合成联合查询嵌入，需要针对任务进行专门训练，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。作者希望利用已有的预训练生成模型和大语言模型，以训练自由的方式桥接模态鸿沟，消除对任务特定训练模块的依赖。

Method: 提出PeFuse（伪融合）框架，利用预训练的扩散模型和MLLM进行生成式跨模态转换。具体提出两种策略：单向转换（uni-directional）和双向转换（bi-directional），将CIR分解为四个单模态检索子问题，包括模态内检索和跨模态单查询检索任务。所有组件均可替换，保持高灵活性。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，将CIR转换为文本到图像检索任务比其他转换策略更有效，取得了与最先进方法竞争或更优的性能。伪融合范式在零样本CIR场景中展现出有效性。

Conclusion: PeFuse证明了无需任务特定训练的生成式伪融合范式可以有效解决零样本CIR问题，通过将复杂跨模态组合查询重构为简单的单模态检索任务，在性能与灵活性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is an emerging paradigm in content-based image retrieval that enables users to formulate compositional queries by combining a reference image with an auxiliary modality, usually text-based. This approach supports fine-grained search where the target image shares structural elements with the user-provided image while incorporating the modifications specified by the auxiliary text. Conventional CIR methods rely on multimodal fusion to combine visual and textual features into a joint query embedding, which requires training modules that align composed queries with the targets. In this work, we propose PeFuse (for pseudo-fusion), a training-free framework that leverages pretrained Diffusion Models and Multimodal Large Language Models to bridge modalities via generative conversion. We introduce two novel strategies: uni-directional and bi-directional conversion, which convert CIR into four single-modality retrieval problems. These methods reformulate CIR as either intra-modal or cross-modal single-query retrieval tasks, bypassing the need for dedicated task-specific training. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that converting CIR into text-to-image retrieval tasks is more effective than alternative conversion strategies, achieving competitive or superior performance compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining high flexibility thanks to replaceable components of the conversion pipeline. These results highlight the effectiveness of the pseudo-fusion paradigm for zero-shot CIR. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/StevenXuf/PeFuse4CIR.

</details>


### [25] [DF-MoE: Generalizable Deepfake Detection via Multimodal Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23363)
*Vlad Hondru,Florinel Alin Croitoru,Iuliana Georgescu,A. Sophia Koepke,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-MoE框架，通过集成多种预训练模型提取音频和视觉模态的多种高层线索（口部运动、面部解析、表情、头部姿态、视线追踪、心率、音频情感和语音活动），并使用Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）骨干网络融合单模态和多模态线索进行音视频深度伪造检测。在五个基准数据集上的域内和跨域实验中，DF-MoE超越所有现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频深度伪造检测的主要挑战是开发能够跨伪造生成方法泛化的检测器。作者推测可以通过预训练模型从音频和视觉模态中提取多种高层线索来缓解过拟合问题，从而提升检测器的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 组装多种预训练模型，提取包括口部运动、面部解析、面部表情、头部姿态、视线追踪、心率、音频情感和语音活动在内的丰富特征；2) 通过Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）骨干网络集成单模态和多模态线索进行深度伪造检测。

Result: 在MAVOS-DD、AVLips、PolyGlotFake、BioDeepAV、FakeAVCeleb五个基准数据集上的域内和跨域实验中，DF-MoE取得了优于所有竞争方法（state-of-the-art方法）的深度伪造检测结果。

Conclusion: DF-MoE通过利用多种预训练模型提取多样化高层线索并采用MoE架构进行特征融合，有效缓解了过拟合问题，显著提升了音视频深度伪造检测的准确性和跨方法泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-visual deepfake detection is an actively studied topic, where one of the main challenges is to develop detectors able to generalize across deepfake generation methods. We conjecture that overfitting can be mitigated by extracting multiple high-level cues from the available audio and visual modalities via pre-trained models. We therefore assemble a wide variety of pre-trained models to extract features that encode mouth movements, face parsing, facial expressions, head pose, gaze tracking, heart rate, audio emotion and speech activity. We further integrate both unimodal and multimodal cues via a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) backbone to detect deepfakes. We perform in-domain and cross-domain experiments on five benchmarks for deepfake detection (MAVOS-DD, AVLips, PolyGlotFake, BioDeepAV, FakeAVCeleb) to compare our framework (DF-MoE) with state-of-the-art methods. Our results indicate that DF-MoE obtains superior deepfake detection results, surpassing all competing methods. We release our code at https://github.com/vladhondru25/DF-MoE.

</details>


### [26] [SAFE-G: Structure-aware Faithful Evidence-guided Generation for Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21796)
*Long Shu,Shuochen Liu,Wei Chen,Junda Lin,Zhi Zheng,Huijun Hou,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFE-G框架，通过结构感知的细粒度图检索和基于证据奖励的强化学习策略，解决知识型视觉问答中证据定位不精准和推理不可信的问题，在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek基准上分别提升8.9%和3.5%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA方法虽然在多模态特征融合和外部知识检索方面取得进展，但存在两个关键缺陷：一是难以捕捉复杂上下文中的结构关联以有效过滤噪声，二是无法保证推理过程严格忠实于检索到的证据。

Method: SAFE-G采用两阶段检索与强化学习推理相结合的策略：(1) 使用融合视觉和文本模态的粗粒度混合搜索召回候选文档；(2) 实施结构感知的细粒度图检索，捕获结构依赖关系以过滤噪声并精确定位证据；(3) 引入带证据加权奖励的强化学习策略，仅当所选证据正确时才为正确答案赋予奖励，强制模型锚定在检索上下文中进行忠实推理。

Result: 在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek两个基准数据集上，SAFE-G分别以8.9%和3.5%的幅度超越现有方法，显著提升了整体推理准确率。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知的证据定位与严格的证据对齐约束，SAFE-G有效增强了多模态特征下的证据定位能力和忠实推理能力，验证了结构信息与证据约束在知识型视觉问答中的关键价值。

Abstract: Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) aims to answer queries that necessitate reasoning over external knowledge sources beyond the visual content. Typically, current methods fuse multimodal features to retrieve external information, subsequently leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to derive answers from the retrieved evidence. However, these methods often struggle to capture structural associations within complex contexts to effectively filter noise. Furthermore, they frequently fail to ensure that the reasoning process remains strictly faithful to the retrieved evidence. To address these challenges, we propose SAFE-G, a Structure-Aware Faithful Evidence-guided Generation framework, which enables precise evidence localization and trustworthy reasoning. Specifically, we first employ a coarse-grained hybrid search fusing visual and textual modalities to recall candidate documents, and subsequently implement a structure-aware fine-grained graph retrieval that captures structural dependencies to filter noise and pinpoint precise evidence. Moreover, we introduce a reinforcement learning (RL) strategy with an evidence-grounded reward that assigns credit to correct answers only when the selected evidence is correct. This strict alignment constraint compels the model to anchor its response in the retrieved context, effectively enhancing its capability to locate evidence via multimodal features and perform faithful reasoning. Extensive experiments on the Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek benchmarks demonstrate that SAFE-G outperforms prior methods by a margin of 8.9% and 3.5%, substantially enhancing the overall reasoning accuracy. Our source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/MINE-USTC/SAFE-G.

</details>


### [27] [SAM3Dual: A 3rd Place Solution to the MOSEv2 Track, 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22193)
*JeongRae Kim,Chaehyun Kim,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM3Dual是ECCV 2026 LSVOS挑战赛MOSEv2赛道第三名的解决方案，它作为SAM 3的免训练推理扩展，将时间记忆显式划分为短期与长期两个分支，并通过确定性融合调度和前一帧目标置信度调制来组合记忆响应。所有预训练参数保持冻结，无需任何训练或微调，最终获得官方J&F 64.37的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期视频目标分割（VOS）任务中，如何在不进行任务特定训练的前提下有效组织时间记忆以提升性能，是当前研究的核心挑战。作者希望探索在推理阶段重新组织时间记忆结构的可行性，从而在保持预训练模型不变的同时获得具有竞争力的长期VOS性能。

Method: SAM3Dual将时间记忆拆分为双分支结构：短期分支处理最近观测帧，长期分支采用区间采样方式保留历史表征。两个分支的记忆响应通过确定性的序列相对融合调度进行组合，并用前一帧目标置信度做保守调制。系统完全冻结SAM 3预训练参数，不依赖任何训练、微调、测试时训练或在线参数优化。

Result: 在MOSEv2赛道取得官方J&F分数64.37，排名第三。

Conclusion: 该结果证明了完全在推理阶段重组时间记忆即可获得具有竞争力的长期VOS性能，同时保留预训练模型，展示了免训练记忆组织的潜力。

Abstract: We present SAM3Dual, our third-place solution to the MOSEv2 track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge at ECCV 2026. SAM3Dual is a training-free inference extension of pretrained SAM 3 that explicitly separates temporal memory into a short-term branch for recent observations and a long-term branch for interval-sampled historical representations. The two memory responses are combined using a deterministic sequence-relative fusion schedule and conservatively modulated by the previous-frame object confidence. All pretrained SAM 3 parameters remain frozen, requiring no task-specific training, fine-tuning, test-time training, or online parameter optimization. The complete system achieved an official J&F score of 64.37 and ranked third in the MOSEv2 track. This result highlights the potential of reorganizing temporal memory entirely at inference time to obtain competitive long-term VOS performance while preserving the pretrained model.

</details>


### [28] [Long-Horizon Audio-Visual Generation for Persistent Stories and Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23383)
*Nan Duan,Haoyang Huang,Weiyang Jin,Haoran Li,Yaowei Li,Yuming Li,Yijun Liu,Xin Lu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yanwen Ma,Yaofeng Su,Yilang Sun,Haoyu Wang,Zeyue Xue,Songchun Zhang,Junhao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍JoyAI-Echo-1.5，一个统一的音视频生成系统，包含两个变体：长视频变体通过可组合跨镜头记忆和说话者线索实现角色外观与语音身份的持久性；世界模型变体通过几何感知条件路径将异构导航输入转换为6-DoF相机轨迹，实现控制器无关的交互。训练采用渐进式教师强制和短/长时域Self-Gradient Forcing，实验超越长视频基线，并在WBench（81.7分）和SANA-WM-Bench上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成正从孤立片段向长叙事和交互式世界发展，需要模型保持身份一致性、遵循用户控制，并在长时间展开中保持稳定。现有方法在跨镜头角色一致性、用户可控交互和长时程生成稳定性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出JoyAI-Echo-1.5统一音视频生成系统：(1)长视频变体采用可组合跨镜头记忆，聚合多镜头视觉证据，并结合语音过滤的全镜头音频提取说话者线索；支持文本、图像和记忆条件的灵活组合。(2)世界模型变体将异构导航输入转换为校准的度量6-DoF相机轨迹，通过几何感知条件路径注入。(3)将双向音视频骨干转换为因果少步生成器，采用渐进式教师强制及短/长时域Self-Gradient Forcing在自生成展开上进行训练。

Result: JoyAI-Echo-1.5在长视频跨镜头一致性、视觉质量、文本对齐和语音保真度上超越现有基线；世界模型变体在WBench以81.7平均分排名第一，并在SANA-WM-Bench上取得领先的视觉质量和长时程持久性。

Conclusion: 记忆机制、几何控制和展开感知训练为生成连贯故事和持续演化的交互世界提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Video generation is progressing beyond isolated clips toward long-form narratives and interactive worlds, requiring models to preserve identities, follow user controls, and remain stable over extended rollouts. We present JoyAI-Echo-1.5, a unified audio-visual generation system with two purpose-built variants. The long-video variant introduces composable cross-shot memory that aggregates visual evidence across multiple prior shots and speaker cues derived from speech-filtered full-shot audio, enabling persistent character appearance and voice identity across flexible combinations of text, image, and memory conditioning. The world-model variant converts heterogeneous navigation inputs into calibrated metric 6-DoF camera trajectories and injects them through a geometry-aware conditioning pathway, enabling controller-agnostic interaction across flexible viewpoints. To support efficient long-horizon generation, we transform a bidirectional audio-visual backbone into a causal few-step generator using progressive teacher forcing and short- and long-horizon Self-Gradient Forcing on self-generated rollouts. Experiments demonstrate strong performance in both settings. JoyAI-Echo-1.5 achieves improvements over existing long-video baselines in cross-shot consistency, visual quality, text alignment, and speech fidelity. Its world-model variant ranks first on WBench, with an average score of 81.7, and achieves leading visual quality and long-horizon persistence on SANA-WM-Bench. Together, these results indicate that memory, geometric control, and rollout-aware training provide a practical foundation for generating coherent stories and continuously evolving interactive worlds. Project page: https://echo-team-joy-future-academy-jd.github.io/Echo-1.5-Page/.

</details>


### [29] [E2S-Pruner: Progressive Two-Stage Evidence Fusion for Visual Token Pruning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23253)
*Taoyu Qian,Qi Wang,Daqian Shi,Yuanhao Jiang,Shang Gao,Hualong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出E2S-Pruner，一种无需辅助模型、可训练参数或微调的两阶段证据融合框架，用于视觉token剪枝。第一阶段将每个注意力头视为独立证据源，估计其可靠性并用三种状态（重要、不重要、不确定）表征每个视觉token；第二阶段利用Dempster-Shafer证据理论量化层间冲突并融合多层互补证据，同时引入空间新颖性约束防止保留token过度集中。在LLaVA-1.5-7B上保持98.0%、96.8%和90.6%的聚合性能（分别为192、128和64个保留token），并在128和64 token设置下分别提升1.96x和2.09x吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型通常将图像编码为数百个视觉token，导致推理延迟和GPU内存开销显著。现有的剪枝方法主要依赖注意力分数并直接跨注意力头和网络层聚合输出，难以刻画证据不确定性和冲突，因此需要更鲁棒的token剪枝方法。

Method: 提出两阶段证据融合框架E2S-Pruner：(1) 第一阶段将每个注意力头视为独立证据源，从证据清晰度和头间一致性估计其可靠性，并为每个视觉token分配重要、不重要和不确定三个状态；(2) 第二阶段使用Dempster-Shafer证据理论量化层间冲突并融合来自多个网络层的互补证据；(3) 引入空间新颖性约束，促进对不同图像区域的覆盖，防止保留token集中在一小片局部显著区域。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，E2S-Pruner在保留192、128和64个视觉token时分别保持98.0%、96.8%和90.6%的聚合性能，在128和64 token设置下吞吐量分别提升1.96x和2.09x。在Qwen2-VL-7B上的实验进一步验证了跨模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: E2S-Pruner通过证据理论框架有效处理了视觉token剪枝中的不确定性和冲突问题，在不引入额外参数或微调的情况下实现了高效的token剪枝，且展现出跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models typically encode an image into hundreds of visual tokens, incurring substantial inference latency and GPU memory overhead. Existing pruning methods largely rely on attention scores and directly aggregate outputs across attention heads and network layers, making it difficult to characterize evidential uncertainty and conflict. We propose E2S-Pruner, a progressive two-stage evidence-fusion framework for visual token pruning that requires no auxiliary model, trainable parameters, or fine-tuning. In the first stage, E2S-Pruner treats each attention head as an independent evidence source, estimates its reliability from evidence clarity and inter-head consistency, and represents each visual token using three states: important, unimportant, and uncertain. In the second stage, Dempster--Shafer evidence theory is used to quantify inter-layer conflict and fuse complementary evidence from multiple network layers. We further introduce a spatial novelty constraint that promotes coverage of distinct image regions and prevents the retained tokens from concentrating in a few locally salient areas. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, E2S-Pruner retains 98.0%, 96.8%, and 90.6% of the aggregate performance when the average numbers of retained visual tokens are 192, 128, and 64, respectively, while improving throughput by 1.96x and 2.09x under the 128-token and 64-token settings. Experiments on Qwen2-VL-7B further demonstrate cross-model generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/taoyu-qian/E2S-Pruner.git.

</details>


### [30] [Mover360: Controllable Object Manipulation in 360° Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23238)
*Haoyi Zhong,Fang-Lue Zhang,Andrew Chalmers,Taehyun Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mover360是一个针对360°全景图像的受控对象操作框架，通过统一的点/框/掩码引导界面和ERP对齐的指令图，实现了对象平移（核心）、参考引导插入和移除等操作。它基于轻量化的预训练扩散Transformer，并利用UE5数据生成管线构建大规模配对数据与双域基准，在重建保真度、语义一致性和分布质量上优于多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透视图像编辑器难以处理360°等距矩形投影（ERP）图像中的对象级编辑，原因在于360°图像具有水平环绕、纬度相关畸变和全局场景连续性等特性，且用户也难以精确指定编辑需求。现有方法无法应对这些独特挑战，因此需要一个专门面向360°图像的可控对象操作框架。

Method: Mover360将对象翻译（在现有全景中重定位指定对象）作为核心任务，同时支持参考引导的插入和移除。其界面通过将每个任务编码为固定提示词和紧凑的ERP对齐指令图，统一了点、边界框和掩码三种引导方式（默认点模式下单击即可重定位对象，由模型利用全景上下文和辅助深度条件推断合理大小、支撑和光照）。结构上，它是一个预训练扩散Transformer的轻量化适配。为生成配对监督，构建了UE5数据生成管线，包含表面感知对象放置和随机光照，产出大规模配对数据以及一个合成+真实全景的双域基准。

Result: 在两个测试域和两种评估协议下，Mover360在重建保真度、语义一致性和分布质量方面均优于透视编辑、插入和修复任务的强基线方法。

Conclusion: Mover360证明了通过统一的交互界面和ERP对齐的指令设计，可以有效地实现360°全景图像中的可控对象操作，并提供了开源代码与基准数据集以推动该领域后续研究。

Abstract: We present Mover360, a controllable object manipulation framework for 360° images. Unlike perspective images, 360° images in equirectangular projection (ERP) exhibit horizontal wrap-around, latitude-dependent distortion, and global scene continuity, which makes object-level edits difficult for existing perspective editors to produce and for users to specify. To address this, Mover360 centers on object Translation (relocating a specified object within an existing panorama) while supporting reference-guided Insert and Remove as auxiliary tasks. Its interface unifies point-, bbox-, and mask-guided control by encoding each task into a fixed prompt and a compact, ERP-aligned instruction map. In the default point mode, a single click relocates an object, allowing the model to infer a plausible size, support, and illumination using panoramic context and an auxiliary depth condition. Structurally, Mover360 is a lightweight adaptation of a pretrained diffusion transformer. To generate paired supervision, we construct a UE5 data-generation pipeline with surface-aware object placement and randomized illumination, yielding large-scale paired data and a dual-domain benchmark of synthetic and real panoramas with ground truth for all three tasks. Across both test domains and two evaluation protocols, Mover360 outperforms strong baselines for perspective editing, insertion, and inpainting in reconstruction fidelity, semantic consistency, and distributional quality. Code and our benchmark dataset are available at https://zhonghaoyi.github.io/Mover360/.

</details>


### [31] [DECO: Depth-Guided Co-Visibility Reasoning for Low-Altitude UAV Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22289)
*Yibin Ye,Xichao Teng,Shuo Chen,Xiaokai Song,Dongdong Guan,Qifeng Yu,Zhang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DECO是一个面向低空无人机视觉定位的深度引导共可见性推理框架。它利用单目深度先验推断局部表面几何，估计无人机图像与参考正射地图之间的共可见区域，并通过几何-显著性耦合的共可见性评分对关键点进行排序，保留既视觉独特又在几何上共可见的关键点，从而提升特征匹配和PnP位姿估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS受限环境下，无人机常通过匹配关键点与地理参考正射地图来估计位姿。然而参考地图主要记录俯视表面（如屋顶、地面），竖直结构（如立面、墙壁）被压缩或缺失，导致低空无人机图像中许多视觉上独特的关键点在参考地图中没有有效对应点，造成冗余匹配和位姿估计不准确。

Method: DECO框架包含三个核心组件：1）利用单目深度先验推断局部表面几何；2）估计无人机图像与参考地图之间的共可见区域；3）引入几何-显著性耦合共可见性评分（Geometry-Saliency Coupled Co-visibility Score），联合考虑几何共可见性与检测器显著性进行关键点排序，从而滤除不可见关键点，保留兼具视觉独特性和几何共可见性的关键点，提升匹配与PnP位姿求解。

Result: 广泛实验表明DECO实现了优越的定位性能，且可与不同的深度模型、特征检测器和匹配器集成使用，具有良好的泛化性和兼容性。

Conclusion: DECO通过深度引导的共可见性推理有效解决了低空无人机图像与正射参考地图之间的几何不一致问题，显著提升了视觉定位精度，并作为通用框架可灵活适配多种现有深度估计和特征匹配方法。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly require robust visual localization in GNSS-denied environments. A common solution estimates UAV poses by matching keypoints between UAV images and geo-tagged orthographic reference maps derived from satellite or aerial imagery, followed by Perspective-\(n\)-Point (PnP) pose solving. However, such reference maps mainly record top-down surfaces such as roofs and ground planes, while vertical structures such as facades and walls are often compressed or missing. Consequently, many visually distinctive keypoints in low-altitude UAV images have no valid counterparts in the reference map, leading to redundant matches and inaccurate pose estimation. To address this issue, we propose DECO, a DEpth-guided CO-visibility reasoning framework for low-altitude UAV visual localization. DECO uses monocular depth priors to infer local surface geometry and estimate co-visible regions between UAV images and the reference map. Based on this prior, a Geometry-Saliency Coupled Co-visibility Score is introduced to jointly consider geometric co-visibility and detector saliency for keypoint ranking. In this way, DECO retains keypoints that are both visually distinctive and geometrically co-visible, improving feature matching and PnP-based pose estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECO achieves superior localization performance and can be integrated with different depth models, feature detectors, and matchers. The source code will be available at https://github.com/UAV-AVL/DECO.

</details>


### [32] [Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency (FDC): Data Pruning for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21754)
*Dongyao Zhu,Ranga Raju Vatsavai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将数据剪枝（DP）方法应用于遥感变化检测任务，系统评估了六种代表性DP方法，发现现有基线方法相比随机选择并无可靠优势。通过540个随机数据子集的回归分析，揭示了"变化分布保真度"（change distribution fidelity）是决定变化检测数据子集质量的最关键因素，并据此提出FDC（Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency）两阶段剪枝方法，在多个变化检测基准和骨干网络上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据剪枝在分类和分割任务中已展现减少训练数据并提升模型性能的潜力，但其在遥感变化检测中的潜力尚未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地评估DP方法在该任务上的表现，并理解其失效的根本原因。

Method: （1）在建筑和森林变化检测数据集上，对六种代表性DP方法在CNN和Transformer骨干网络及三种剪枝预算下进行基准测试；（2）对540个随机采样的数据子集进行系统回归分析，用标签统计、图像多样性和特征空间几何四类描述符刻画子集，并训练随机森林模型识别关键因素；（3）基于发现提出FDC（Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency）两阶段剪枝方法，将变化分布保真度、像素级图像多样性和标签-特征一致性三个因素结合。

Result: 现有DP基线在变化检测中无可靠优势，最强的Feature Diversity基线甚至被约33%的随机子集匹配或超越；随机森林回归显示变化分布保真度是最重要的决定因素，像素级图像多样性和标签-特征一致性为次要因素；FDC方法在变化检测基准和骨干网络上一致优于现有基线，尤其在较低剪枝比例下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 数据剪枝在变化检测任务中需要专门设计，现有通用方法忽视的"变化分布保真度"是关键所在。FDC方法有效利用了这一发现，为遥感变化检测领域提供了一种简单且高效的剪枝策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite the success of data pruning (DP) in reducing training data sizes and improving downstream model performance in classification and segmentation tasks, its potential in remote sensing change detection remains unexplored. For the first time, we benchmark six representative DP methods across building- and forest-change datasets, CNN- and transformer-based models, and three pruning budgets, and show that existing baselines yield no reliable advantage over random selection. Notably, even the strongest evaluated baseline, Feature Diversity, is matched or exceeded by $\sim$33\% of randomly sampled subsets. To understand the underlying mechanism, we conduct a systematic regression study over 540 randomly sampled data subsets, characterizing each with four descriptors covering label statistics, image diversity, and feature-space geometry. Random Forest models show that \emph{change distribution fidelity} is the most prominent factor in determining the quality of change detection data subsets, a property absent from the existing pruning literature. Our analyses further show that pixel-wise image diversity and label-feature consistency are secondary factors. We translate these findings into Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency (FDC), a simple two-stage pruning method that shows consistent improvements over existing baselines across change detection benchmarks and backbones, especially at lower pruning ratios. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/ddydyd32/fidelity-diversity-consistency}{https://github.com/ddydyd32/fidelity-diversity-consistency}.

</details>


### [33] [Loopy: Seamless Video Loop Generation via Anchored Looping Shift of Positional Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23090)
*Haotian Dong,Wenjing Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Xin Wang,Di Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Loopy框架，首次揭示DiT中不同注意力层的位置嵌入具有不同的时序控制强度，其中最显著的层作为"锚点"层，通过锚定位置嵌入平移策略，将DiT的时序感知从直线变为圆环，实现高质量无缝循环视频生成，支持RGB和RGBA视频并兼具身份控制和风格迁移等AIGC功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环视频在网页图形、游戏开发、社交媒体等实际应用中至关重要，但现有方法因忽略视频生成模型如何感知时序顺序及其与循环行为的关联，难以生成高质量的循环视频。

Method: 提出锚定位置嵌入平移策略：首先识别DiT各注意力层中位置控制最强的"锚点"层，将其作为循环视频的参考点，为其余层提供上下文先验；然后根据每层时序控制效果分配不同的层专属平移长度，将DiT的时间感知从直线变换为圆形，从而生成无缝循环视频。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提升了生成循环视频的时序一致性和视觉保真度，支持RGB和RGBA视频生成，同时支持身份控制和风格迁移等高级AIGC功能，模型已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将位置嵌入的锚点层作为循环参考点，并采用层专属的平移策略来变换时序感知，能够有效生成高质量、无缝衔接的循环视频。

Abstract: Looping videos are essential for practical applications such as web graphics, game development, and social media. However, existing approaches typically fail to generate high-quality looping videos due to the neglect of how video generation models perceive temporal order and how this relates to the looping behavior. In this work, we are the first to reveal that position embedding at different attention layers within DiT exhibits varying levels of positional control, with the most pronounced layer acting as an anchor. We formulate this anchored layer as the reference point of the looping video, offering strong contextual priors for the remaining layers to facilitate the generation of seamless and coherent video content. Based on this insight, we propose an anchored position embedding shifting strategy that applies layer-specific shift lengths according to each layer's temporal control effect, effectively transforming DiT's temporal perception from a straight line to a circle. Leveraging this strategy, we develop a general framework, Loopy, for high-quality looping video generation, supporting both RGB and RGBA videos, while also enabling advanced AIGC features such as identity control and style transfer. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves temporal consistency and visual fidelity in generated looping videos. The released model is available on our website: https://donghaotian123.github.io/Loopy.

</details>


### [34] [Adapting Dense Vision-Language Relationships for Multi-label Classification with Partial Label](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22313)
*Cheng Chen,Yifan Zhao,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LDSA（Language-driven Dense Semantic Adaptor），一种用于不完整标注的多标签图像分类方法，通过从CLIP多模态预训练模型中挖掘先验自适应关系，利用密集对比适配器和语言驱动的交互解码器，在公开基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完整标注的多标签图像分类任务虽高效但困难，现有方法依赖强统计先验来恢复缺失语义，但这些统计先验存在不稳定语义错误并导致灾难性过拟合问题。

Method: 提出LDSA框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 密集对比适配器，通过构造密集视觉对比约束将任务特定知识迁移到视觉域；(2) 语言驱动交互解码器，利用类别特定提示调优使语言代理与视觉域协同适配。两个模块协作学习实现先验自适应。

Result: 在公开多标签分类基准数据集上达到新的最先进水平，可解释性分析表明LDSA通过先验自适应学习方案发现了隐式语义关系。

Conclusion: LDSA通过利用CLIP多模态预训练模型的先验自适应关系，有效解决了不完整标注多标签分类中的过拟合问题，验证了语言-视觉协同学习在该任务中的有效性和可解释性。

Abstract: Learning multi-label image classification with incomplete annotations is a challenging task that has been widely studied for its superior trade-off between high efficiency and less labor consumption on large-scale datasets. Predominant methods rely on strong prior assumptions to recover the missing semantics from partial annotations. However, these statistic priors suffer from unstable semantic mistakes and thus lead to catastrophic overfitting. Toward this end, we propose a Language-driven Dense Semantic Adaptor (LDSA) that excavates prior-adaptive relationships from multimodal pretrained CLIP models. In our approach, the densely contrastive adaptor is first proposed to construct dense visual contrastive constraints, transferring the task-specific knowledge to visual domains.
