We then propose a language-driven interactive decoder with the help of class-specific prompt tuning, which adapts language proxies with visual domains. With the collaborative learning of proposed modules, experimental results demonstrate our proposed LDSA achieves a new state of the art on public multi-label classification benchmarks, and interpretable analyses reveal that our LDSA discovers implicit semantic relationships with the prior-adaptive learning scheme.

</details>


### [35] [AquaFlow: A Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Underwater Streaming Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22906)
*Yingxiang Xu,Kerui Ren,Wenqi Guo,Changjian Jiang,Tao Lu,Linning Xu,Mulin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AquaFlow是一个面向水下场景的单目3D高斯溅射(3DGS)流式重建框架，通过微调3D视觉基础模型实现鲁棒的姿态和点图估计，并引入介质引导的增量高斯初始化和混合场景表示（结构化的距离条件神经高斯与物理启发的光学模型相结合），在62条水下轨迹数据集上实现了SOTA的跟踪和渲染性能，相比WaterSplat-SLAM平均定位误差降低13.2%，PSNR提升4.74 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单目3DGS流式重建方法在常规场景中表现优异，但直接扩展到水下场景时面临严重挑战：光衰减和散射引起视觉退化，导致相机姿态跟踪精度下降和场景几何失真。因此需要专门针对水下物理特性的流式重建框架。

Method: 1) 在大型水下数据上微调3D视觉基础模型，实现鲁棒的姿态和点图估计；2) 提出介质引导的增量高斯初始化策略用于流式建图；3) 开发流式兼容的混合场景表示，将结构化的距离条件神经高斯与物理启发的光学模型集成，补偿水下图像形成效应。

Result: 在62条水下轨迹（涵盖公共基准和野外网络视频，多尺度场景）上进行了全面评估，AquaFlow在跟踪和渲染方面均达到SOTA：相比WaterSplat-SLAM平均定位误差降低13.2%，PSNR提升4.74 dB。

Conclusion: AquaFlow通过结合水下物理光学建模与3DGS流式重建技术，有效解决了水下场景的视觉退化问题，实现了高效且高保真的水下场景重建，验证了物理感知建模对复杂退化环境下重建性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) streaming reconstruction methods have achieved impressive performance by balancing reconstruction quality and efficiency. However, extending these frameworks to underwater scenes remains challenging due to severe visual degradation, such as light attenuation and scattering, which degrades camera pose tracking and distorts scene geometry. To address these challenges, we propose AquaFlow, a monocular Gaussian Splatting streaming reconstruction framework for efficient and high-fidelity underwater reconstruction. Specifically, AquaFlow fine-tunes a 3D vision foundation model on large-scale underwater data for robust pose and pointmap estimation, and introduces a medium-guided incremental Gaussian initialization strategy for streaming mapping. Furthermore, we develop a streaming-compatible hybrid scene representation that integrates structured, distance-conditioned neural Gaussians with a physics-inspired optical model to compensate for underwater image formation effects, enabling accurate scene reconstruction. We evaluate AquaFlow on a comprehensive dataset of 62 diverse underwater trajectories, collected from both public benchmarks and in-the-wild web videos across various scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AquaFlow achieves state-of-the-art tracking and rendering performance, reducing average localization error by 13.2% and improving PSNR by 4.74 dB compared to WaterSplat-SLAM.

</details>


### [36] [Boosting Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering with Structured Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21431)
*Qiyou Liu,Yong Zhang,Jianjie Luo,Zhenguo Yang,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCoRe框架，通过三阶段（上下文获取、上下文选择、上下文压缩）将异构视觉描述和知识转化为结构化关系三元组，解决长无结构提示退化推理性能的问题，在OK-VQA和A-OKVQA基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的K-VQA方法依赖上下文学习将多模态描述和知识直接拼接成冗长无结构的提示，导致大量无关上下文存在且缺乏显式关系结构，从而降低LLM的推理性能。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的结构化上下文推理（SCoRe）框架，包含三阶段：1) Context Acquisition：生成多样视觉描述并通过高效的两阶段多模态检索获取显式知识；2) Context Selection：利用LLM引导的选择机制过滤相关的视觉、显式和隐式知识；3) Context Compression：通过关系逻辑蒸馏（RLD）将原始文本转换为显式的实体-关系三元组，作为简洁结构化提示用于最终答案预测。

Result: 在OK-VQA和A-OKVQA基准上的大量实验表明，SCoRe持续超越现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 将异构上下文转化为关系三元组的结构化提示能有效缓解长提示推理退化问题，显著提升知识驱动VQA的性能。

Abstract: Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering aims to answer questions about an image by integrating external knowledge with visual and textual information. Recent approaches often rely on in-context learning to prompt Large Language Models (LLMs) with multimodal context in a zero-shot or few-shot manner. However, we observe that directly concatenating heterogeneous visual descriptions and retrieved knowledge into long, unstructured prompts often degrades reasoning performance, due to both excessive irrelevant context and the lack of explicit relational structure. In this paper, we propose an LLM-based Structured Context Reasoning (SCoRe) framework that infers both explicit and implicit relationships for prediction. SCoRe consists of three stages: Context Acquisition, which generates diverse visual notes and retrieves explicit knowledge via an efficient two-stage multimodal retrieval strategy; Context Selection, which filters relevant visual, explicit, and implicit knowledge using LLM-guided selection; and Context Compression, which performs Relational Logic Distillation (RLD) to transform raw text into explicit entity-relation triplets. These relational triplets serve as a concise and structured prompt for final answer prediction. Extensive experiments on the OK-VQA and A-OKVQA benchmarks demonstrate that SCoRe consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [37] [ByteAction: Byte-space Action Recognition Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22760)
*Fangcheng Li,Zhen Yu,Kejun Wu,Qiong Liu,You Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ByteAction，一个基于字节空间的行动识别基础模型，能够在图像位流损坏的情况下仍实现准确的人类动作识别，通过双视角字节级识别框架、位流模式增强和损坏一致性训练策略，在多个数据集上取得了最先进的损坏鲁棒性，同时保持完整的位流识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动作识别依赖像素解码，但在隐私敏感场景和位流损坏情况下不适用。字节空间动作识别（BAR）直接在压缩图像位流上操作，天然具有隐私保护和位流完整性无关的特性，但现有方法在损坏位流上的鲁棒性不足，需要专门设计。

Method: ByteAction采用双视角字节级识别框架：(1) 构建弱损坏和强损坏两个位流视角，通过位流模式增强（BPA）进行数据增强，将一维字节序列重塑为二维字节矩阵并应用区域级擦除以学习跨区域字节依赖；(2) 使用共享的ByteFormer骨干网络编码；(3) 通过分类损失和损坏一致性训练目标进行优化，后者使用双向KL散度约束模型在不同损坏程度下保持预测稳定。

Result: 在Stanford40、PPMI和PASCAL VOC 2012 Action的图像位流上进行的实验表明，ByteAction在所有场景下均达到最先进的损坏鲁棒性，同时保持具有竞争力的完整位流识别性能。

Conclusion: ByteAction验证了直接从字节空间进行行动识别的可行性，通过特定的字节级增强和一致性约束，实现了对位流损坏的高鲁棒性，为隐私敏感和低带宽场景下的动作识别提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Byte-space Action Recognition (BAR) aims to recognize human actions directly from compressed image bitstreams without any pixel decoding. By operating entirely in byte space, BAR is inherently independent of file integrity and pixel-level reconstruction, making it naturally applicable to privacy-sensitive scenarios and robust against bitstream corruption. In this paper, we propose ByteAction, a BAR foundation model that achieves accurate action recognition on corrupted image bitstreams. ByteAction follows a dual-view byte-level recognition framework. It constructs weakly and strongly corrupted bitstream views, which are augmented by Bitstream Pattern Augmentation (BPA) and encoded with a shared ByteFormer backbone. The model is optimized with both classification and corruption consistency objectives. Specifically, we propose Bitstream Pattern Augmentation (BPA), which reshapes one-dimensional byte sequences into two-dimensional byte matrix and applies region-level erasure to encourage the model to learn robust cross-region byte dependencies. We further propose a Corruption Consistency Training strategy that constrains the model to maintain stable predictions across different corruption severities through bidirectional KL divergence. Experiments on the image bitstream from Stanford40, PPMI, and PASCAL VOC 2012 Action demonstrate that ByteAction achieves state-of-the-art corruption robustness across all scenarios while maintaining competitive intact bitstream performance.

</details>


### [38] [C$^2$Path: Class-Conditional Pathway Decoupling for Vision-Language Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21937)
*Lecheng Xu,Feifei Shao,Ouyangzi Ye,Zhen Wang,Lin Li,Kexin Li,Zhao Wang,Changqin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出C²Path，一个面向视觉语言增量目标检测的类条件通路解耦框架，通过类别专家库和类条件解耦模块生成类感知路由信号，动态组合ClassLoRA适配器，为不同类别建立独立可更新的计算通路，解决类别边界侵蚀和类别表征纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量目标检测方法存在两种形式的类别知识耦合：共享参数更新导致的类别边界侵蚀，以及混合特征编码引发的类别表征纠缠。作者认为有效的增量学习需要类特定的计算通路，实现隔离的参数更新和分离的类别注入。

Method: C²Path由两个核心组件构成：(1) 类别专家库，包含可学习的低秩计算节点，捕获类别特定知识；(2) 类条件解耦模块，利用token级类别线索生成类感知路由信号，动态组合ClassLoRA适配器，为不同类别构建独立的计算通路，实现隔离更新和分离注入。

Result: 在COCO 2017数据集上的多种增量学习设置下进行了大量实验，C²Path一致性地优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在视觉语言检测器中持续类别扩展的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: C²Path通过类特定的解耦计算通路有效解决了增量目标检测中的类别知识耦合问题，为视觉语言检测器的持续类别扩展提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Incremental Object Detection (IOD) aims to enable detectors to continuously learn novel categories while preserving previously acquired knowledge. However, existing methods suffer from two forms of \textbf{class knowledge coupling}: class boundary erosion induced by shared parameter updates and class representation entanglement arising from mixed feature encoding. We argue that effective incremental learning requires class-specific computational pathways that enable isolated parameter updates and separated class-wise injection. To this end, we propose \textbf{C$^2$Path}, a class-conditional pathway decoupling framework for vision-language incremental object detection that leverages token-level class cues to establish dedicated and updatable computational pathways for different categories. Specifically, C$^2$Path introduces a category expert library and a class-conditional decoupling module. The expert library consists of learnable low-rank computational nodes that capture category-specific knowledge, while the decoupling module generates class-aware routing signals to dynamically compose \textit{ClassLoRA} adapters from these experts, thereby forming class-specific computational pathways for isolated updates and separated injection across categories. Extensive experiments on COCO 2017 under multiple incremental learning settings demonstrate that C$^2$Path consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, providing an effective and scalable solution for continual category expansion in vision-language detectors.

</details>


### [39] [DesignAgent3D: Interactive 3D Scene Editing via Designer-like Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21438)
*Xiujin Liu,Tianyu Yang,Yilun Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DesignAgent3D是一个交互式多模态智能体框架，将3D场景编辑重新定义为"计划-感知-行动"（Plan-Perceive-Act）范式，通过与用户交互澄清模糊意图、在杂乱3D场景中精确定位编辑目标，并保持多视图一致性，在NeRF和3D Gaussian Splatting后端上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本引导3D场景编辑方法通常将任务表述为单次条件生成，无法解决模糊的用户意图或实现精确的空间定位，导致物体定位漂移、遮挡下追踪失败以及多视角"贴纸效应"等严重问题。

Method: 提出Plan-Perceive-Act三段式智能体框架：1) Plan阶段通过与用户交互澄清未明确的设计目标；2) Perceive阶段将编辑意图接地到3D场景中的特定物体或区域；3) Act阶段在保持场景一致性的前提下应用受控视觉修改，编辑结果进一步集成到底层3D表示中，支持持久化和多视图一致的新视角渲染。

Result: 在NeRF和3D Gaussian Splatting两种后端上的大量实验表明，DesignAgent3D在语义意图对齐、空间定位准确性和多视图一致性方面均显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将3D编辑从单次条件生成转变为交互式多模态代理范式，DesignAgent3D有效解决了模糊意图和空间定位难题，为3D场景编辑提供了一种更接近设计师工作流程的解决方案。

Abstract: Text guided 3D scene editing provides an intuitive interface for modifying reconstructed environments, but remains difficult because natural language design requests are often semantically underspecified and must be grounded in cluttered 3D scenes. Existing methods typically formulate the task as one-shot conditional generation from a single prompt, failing to resolve ambiguous user intents or achieve precise spatial grounding. Consequently, they suffer from severe object localization drift, tracking failure under occlusions, and the notorious multi-view "sticker effect." To overcome these limitations, we present DesignAgent3D, an interactive multimodal agentic framework that reformulates 3D scene editing as a designer-like Plan-Perceive-Act paradigm. The agent first plans by interacting with the user to clarify underspecified design goals, then perceives by grounding the intended edit to specific objects or regions in the 3D scene, and finally acts by applying controlled visual modifications while preserving scene consistency. The edits are further integrated into the underlying 3D representation, supporting persistent and multi-view consistent novel-view rendering. Extensive experiments across both NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting backbones demonstrate that DesignAgent3D significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, delivering superior semantic intent alignment, impeccable spatial localization accuracy, and high-fidelity multi-view consistency.

</details>


### [40] [EG-ARSA: An Expert-Grounded Open Model for Visual Road Safety Auditing in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23563)
*Md Thamed Bin Zaman Chowdhury,Moazzem Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出专家接地蒸馏（EGD）框架，将机构道路安全专业知识迁移到紧凑的视觉语言模型中，用于可扩展的可视化道路安全审计。该框架通过量化专家接地阶段校准教师模型（Cohen's kappa=0.74），再将结构化监督蒸馏到80亿参数的LoRA微调学生模型中。同时引入BD-ARSA数据集（21,947条图像审计记录）和EG-ARSA模型。实验表明接地微调显著改善了序数风险评估，紧凑学生模型在盲评中超越了310亿参数的教师和Gemini-2.5-Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家的道路交通伤害是重大挑战，但主动式道路安全审计受限于事故记录不完整、合格审计人员短缺以及大规模现场检查成本高昂。现有方案缺乏可扩展且经济的可视化审计工具，亟需一种能将专业知识自动化、规模化的AI解决方案。

Method: 提出Expert-Grounded Distillation (EGD)框架：(1) 量化专家接地阶段——教师视觉语言模型与权威现场审计校准，达到实质性一致（Cohen's kappa=0.74）后才允许大规模标注；(2) 蒸馏阶段——校准后的教师生成结构化监督，通过LoRA和单一无泄漏提示蒸馏至80亿参数的学生模型；(3) 构建BD-ARSA数据集（21,947条图像审计记录，近全国覆盖）和EG-ARSA模型。

Result: 实验结果显示：接地微调在序数风险评估上显著优于零样本基线；盲评中紧凑学生模型（80亿参数）优于其310亿参数的教师模型和Gemini-2.5-Flash；教师与专家评估达到kappa=0.74的实质性一致性。

Conclusion: EGD为资源受限环境下的大规模主动式道路安全审计提供了有效且可扩展的工程解决方案，展示了知识蒸馏与专家接地结合的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Road traffic injuries remain a major challenge in low- and middle-income countries, where proactive road safety auditing is limited by incomplete crash records, shortages of qualified auditors, and the high cost of large-scale field inspections. To address this problem, we propose Expert-Grounded Distillation (EGD), a novel artificial intelligence framework that transfers institutional road safety expertise into a compact vision-language model for scalable visual road safety auditing. The key innovation is a quantified expert-grounding stage in which the teacher vision-language model is calibrated against authoritative field audits. Large-scale annotation is permitted only after the teacher reaches substantial agreement with expert risk assessments (Cohen's kappa = 0.74). The calibrated teacher then generates structured supervision that is distilled into an 8-billion-parameter student vision-language model using Low-Rank Adaptation and a single leakage-free prompt. We also introduce Bangladesh Road Safety Audit (BD-ARSA), the first open, expert-grounded Bangladeshi visual road safety audit dataset containing 21,947 image-audit records with near-national coverage, and Expert-Grounded Road Safety Auditor (EG-ARSA), the first vision-language model developed specifically for this task. Experimental results show that grounded fine-tuning substantially improves ordinal risk assessment over the zero-shot baseline, while blind expert evaluation demonstrates that the compact student outperforms both its 31 billion-parameter teacher and Gemini-2.5-Flash. These findings demonstrate that EGD provides an effective and scalable engineering solution for proactive road safety auditing in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [41] [Fast and Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting with Polarized Opacity Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22344)
*Zi-Ming Wang,Kai-Wen Duan,Kowei Huang,Akihiro Sugimoto,Shang-Hong Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种高效训练框架，通过L2重建损失和极化不透明度先验（POP）构建内在紧凑的3DGS表示，取代传统的"先密集化再剪枝"循环，在减少高斯数量的同时加速训练并保持渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然渲染质量高且速度快，但存在"模型膨胀"问题——大量冗余的、低不透明度的高斯原语导致内存和训练成本过高。这种低效源于标准的"密集化-剪枝"范式，即先激进扩展模型再依赖剪枝实现紧凑，过程浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出一个协同设计的紧凑训练框架：(1) 使用L2重建损失提供与误差成比例的梯度，稳定优化过程；(2) 提出极化不透明度先验（Polarized Opacity Prior, POP），主动管理高斯群体——将信息丰富的高斯推向完全不透明，将无用高斯推向透明，实现自然剪枝，并通过提前光线终止（Early Ray Termination）加速渲染。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法能持续加速3DGS训练，显著减少所需高斯数量，同时保持可比的视觉重建质量。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为快速且内在紧凑的3DGS训练提供了一条简单而有效的路径。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality at real-time speeds but suffers from "model bloat" - a large number of redundant, low-opacity Gaussians that inflate memory usage and training costs. This inefficiency stems from the standard "densify-then-prune" paradigm, which expands the model aggressively before relying on pruning to achieve compactness. To mitigate this problem, we present an efficient training framework that builds an intrinsically compact representation, replacing the conventional densify-then-prune cycle. Our method leverages a synergistic design: an L2 reconstruction loss to provide error-proportional gradients that stabilize optimization, and a novel Polarized Opacity Prior (POP) to actively manage the Gaussian population. POP steers informative primitives toward full opacity and uninformative ones toward transparency, enabling natural pruning and accelerating rendering through Early Ray Termination. Experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently achieves accelerated 3DGS training with significantly fewer Gaussians while maintaining comparable visual reconstruction quality. These results show that the proposed framework provides a simple and effective path toward fast and inherently compact 3DGS training.

</details>


### [42] [FigmaTrace: Capturing Creative Nuances in Human Figma Design Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21460)
*Darshan Deshpande,Yoshinari Fujinuma,Martyna Markiewicz,Devanshu Bansal,Shivani Jain,Nicholas Saban,Chirag Maheshwari,Anand Kannappan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FigmaTrace数据集，包含由专家策展的126个开放式主观设计任务和超过200小时的人类录屏数据，通过基于设计阶段的方法转化为3469条设计轨迹，训练出的模型在4个分布外GUI环境中达到与Claude-Opus-5和GPT-5.6-Sol等前沿闭源模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在客观可验证任务（如目标检测）上表现良好，但在主观创意设计任务上持续欠佳。主要原因在于缺乏捕捉人类专家多样化偏好和决策过程的高质量人工工作流数据。

Method: 1) 定义专家策展的设计技能与最佳实践分类体系；2) 将其扩展为126个开放式、主观、长时程任务；3) 基于此构建FigmaTrace数据集，包含超过200小时人类录屏数据，通过新颖的设计阶段（design phase-based）方法转化为3469条设计轨迹；4) 使用该数据集训练四个模型，并进行消融实验对比阶段式与长度式轨迹转换方法。

Result: 训练于FigmaTrace的模型在4个分布外agentic GUI环境上的性能提升可与Claude-Opus-5和GPT-5.6-Sol等前沿闭源模型相媲美。消融实验表明基于设计阶段的视频到轨迹转换方法优于此前基于长度的转换方法。对最佳模型Qwen3.8-27B的定性分析进一步关联了性能提升与FigmaTrace数据趋势。

Conclusion: FigmaTrace数据集及其方法显著改善VLM在主观创意设计任务上的表现，数据集和最佳模型已开源供社区使用。设计阶段法优于长度法的发现为未来GUI智能体数据构建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision Language Models have recently shown improvements in several objective and verifiable domains such as object detection but continue to underperform on subjective and creative design tasks. A major contributor to this performance gap is the lack of high quality human workflow data that captures a diverse set of preferences and decisions that make human experts good at design tasks. In this work, we first define a unique, expert curated taxonomy of design skills and best practices which we further expand into a set of 126 open ended, subjective, long horizon tasks. Built on top of this and expert solutions, our dataset FigmaTrace contains over 200 hours of human captured video data converted into 3469 design trajectories using a novel design phase-based method. We use our dataset to train four models and show that training on FigmaTrace leads to a performance improvement comparable to frontier closed models such as \textsc{Claude-Opus-5} and \textsc{GPT-5.6-Sol} on four out of distribution agentic GUI environments. We further perform a useful ablation to attribute these performance improvements to a design phase-based video to trajectory conversion which outperforms prior length-based conversion approaches. Finally, we perform a qualitative analysis on the best performing \textsc{Qwen3.8-27B} outputs to better correlate performance improvements to FigmaTrace's trends. We open source our dataset and the best model for the community.

</details>


### [43] [GuidedFlow: An Attention-Guided Framework for Anomaly Detection in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22789)
*Sosmita Paul,Krishna Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GuidedFlow，一种注意力引导的归一化流模型，用于3D打印中的异常检测与定位，在AM3D-AD和MVTec-AD数据集上优于大多数现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造（3D打印）中质量控制在工业4.0背景下至关重要，但现有异常检测方法在处理3D打印常见的微小/拉丝缺陷时存在泛化能力不足的问题，尤其是在小数据场景下。

Method: GuidedFlow采用预训练的ResNet模型并在领域数据集上微调，通过注意力引导的空间和时间流框架对多尺度和多帧的动力学进行建模，其中时空注意力网络（SAN）使流模型能够优先关注输入帧中的相关上下文线索。

Result: 在自建的AM3D-AD数据集（包含真实3D打印物体图像与视频）以及MVTec-AD工业图像异常检测数据集上的实验表明，GuidedFlow在检测精度和AUROC指标上优于大多数最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的归一化流方法能有效应对3D打印中微小/拉丝缺陷的检测，且在小数据环境下仍保持良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Additive Manufacturing (AM) plays a vital role in the ongoing industrial revolution. However, quality control remains crucial and challenging due to printing defects or potential cyber-physical intrusions. Image or video-based anomaly detection is a key effort towards addressing these challenges. Various approaches have been explored in this domain, including reconstruction-based, embedding-based, and flow-based methods. Though normalizing flow-based methods address some of the core challenges of unforeseen defects and generalization while maintaining detection performance, existing approaches struggle with tiny/stringing defects common in 3D printing. In a small-data setting, this poses a limitation in generalization. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{GuidedFlow}, a novel attention-guided normalizing flow model for anomaly detection and localization. GuidedFlow employs a pre-trained ResNet model, fine-tuned on the domain dataset. An attention-guided spatial and temporal flow framework models the dynamics across multiple scales and frames. A Spatio-Temporal Attention Network (SAN) enables the flow model to prioritize relevant contextual cues from input frames. We evaluate GuidedFlow on our AM3D-AD dataset, consisting of benign and anomalous real 3D printed object images and videos. We also conduct a comparative study using the MVTec-AD industrial image anomaly detection dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that GuidedFlow outperforms most of the state-of-the-art models with enhanced detection accuracy and AUROC.

</details>


### [44] [IntentQA: Intent Question Answering in Videos by Cognitive Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23330)
*Jiapeng Li,Ping Wei,Wenjuan Han,Song-Chun Zhu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IntentQA——一个大规模的、专门针对视频意图推理的VideoQA数据集，并设计了五种由大语言模型生成的对比集及"对比性能下降"指标来评估模型鲁棒性。作者进一步提出X-CaVIR框架，利用三种"认知上下文"（情境、对比和常识）来增强视频理解，并通过透明的LLM集成管线使推理过程可解释。实验证明X-CaVIR在意图推理任务上优于现有基线，且在对比集上表现出良好的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解需要智能体不仅识别视觉事实，还要理解人类行为背后的潜在意图（即社会智能的"暗物质"）。然而现有视频理解任务和数据集缺乏对意图推理的专门建模，且标准评估指标可能因数据集偏差而高估模型能力。

Method: 1) 构建IntentQA大规模视频QA数据集；2) 利用LLM生成五种对比集并引入"Contrast Performance Decline"指标进行鲁棒性评估；3) 提出X-CaVIR框架，整合三种认知上下文：情境上下文（跨模态视频查询语言模块）、对比上下文（对比学习模块）和常识上下文（常识推理模块）；4) 通过透明的LLM集成管线将视频字幕与VQA模型输出协同，实现可解释的推理。

Result: 大量实验证明X-CaVIR各组件有效，整体性能优于最先进基线，且在对比集上对扰动表现出良好的稳定性。

Conclusion: X-CaVIR通过融合三类认知上下文和透明的LLM集成机制，不仅在意图推理任务上取得了SOTA性能，还实现了可解释的推理过程，为视频意图理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Video understanding requires intelligent agents to transcend mere recognition of visual facts and comprehend the underlying intents behind human actions (often termed the "dark matter" of social intelligence). To bridge the gap between visual observation and intent reasoning, we introduce a novel task, IntentQA, and contribute a large-scale VideoQA dataset specifically tailored for this purpose. However, recognizing that standard metrics may overestimate capabilities due to dataset biases, we go beyond simple accuracy to rigorously evaluate model robustness. We augment the benchmark by generating five distinct contrast sets via Large Language Models (LLMs) and introducing a "Contrast Performance Decline" metric. We propose the X-CaVIR (eXplainable Context-aware Video Intent Reasoning) framework, which leverages three types of "Cognitive Context" to enhance video analysis: i) Situational Context via a cross-modal Video Query Language (VQL) module, ii) Contrastive Context via a Contrastive Learning module, and iii) Commonsense Context via a Commonsense Reasoning module. Crucially, to overcome the opacity of traditional black-box models, we refine the integration of LLMs within X-CaVIR by employing a transparent pipeline that synergizes video captions with VQA model outputs. This approach not only improves performance by effectively utilizing rich commonsense knowledge but also renders the reasoning process explicitly interpretable. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our components, the superiority of X-CaVIR over state-of-the-art baselines, and its stability against perturbations on the contrast sets.

</details>

*Xiaopeng Guo,Wai Chung Tse,Yipeng Zhu,Hanwen Zhang,Huajian Huang,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NemoSplat是首个面向水下环境的端到端前馈式4D高斯泼溅（4D Gaussian Splatting）框架，能够直接从无标定的海洋视频中实现媒体感知的动态场景重建。它通过Promptable Dynamic Disentangler分离瞬态动态物体，通过Media-Aware Gaussian Predictor联合估计3D高斯属性和物理介质参数以消除光学衰减，并配套引入大规模水下动态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非受控水下环境中的场景重建面临介质引起的光散射和动态目标干扰两大挑战。现有的前馈式视觉基础模型虽然在新视角合成和跟踪上表现优异，但直接应用于水下视频时，光学衰减和运动干扰会严重破坏其特征聚合，导致跟踪与重建的严重失败，亟需专门针对水下媒体感知的动态重建方案。

Method: NemoSplat包含三个核心组件：(1) 提供稳健的相机姿态估计和密集场景深度估计；(2) Promptable Dynamic Disentangler，通过置信度感知的融合策略，结合学习的动态概率和可选的语义文本先验，有效隔离大规模瞬态实体；(3) Media-Aware Gaussian Predictor，联合估计内在3D高斯属性与物理介质参数，单次前向传播即可渲染无衰减的场景外观。此外，作者构建了包含大量动态元素的大规模水下数据集用于训练与评估。

Result: 在自建的大规模水下动态数据集上进行的大量实验表明，NemoSplat在跟踪精度和渲染质量方面均达到了最先进的水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: NemoSplat验证了通过媒体感知的建模和动态解耦策略，前馈式4D高斯泼溅框架能够有效应对水下复杂光学环境，实现精确的动态场景跟踪与高保真渲染，为水下场景重建领域提供了新的基准方法。

Abstract: Reconstructing photorealistic scenes in unconstrained underwater environments remains challenging due to severe media-induced light scattering and unpredictable dynamic objects. Recent feed-forward visual foundation models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in generalized novel view synthesis and tracking. However, when directly applied to aquatic videos, optical attenuation and motion interference fatally corrupt their feature aggregation, leading to severe tracking and reconstruction failures. To overcome these limitations, we present NemoSplat, the first feed-forward 4D Gaussian Splatting framework tailored for media-aware dynamic reconstruction directly from uncalibrated marine videos. Beyond providing robust estimations of camera poses and dense scene depth, we devise a Promptable Dynamic Disentangler that utilizes a confidence-aware fusion strategy of learned dynamic probabilities and optional semantic text priors, effectively isolating massive transient entities. Furthermore, to counteract visual degradation, a Media-Aware Gaussian Predictor is formulated to jointly estimate intrinsic 3D Gaussian attributes alongside physical media parameters, rendering pristine scene appearance in a single forward pass. Additionally, we introduce a large-scale underwater dataset with massive dynamic elements to facilitate training and evaluation. Extensive experiments on our dataset demonstrate that NemoSplat achieves state-of-the-art tracking accuracy and high-fidelity rendering.

</details>


### [46] [presto: Efficient, Training-free, and Open-world Object Placement via Imaginary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21543)
*Weixuan Ding,Shang Liu,Hanyu Pei,Zeyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出presto，一个零样本、无需训练的框架，将开放世界物体放置重新定义为由多模态大语言模型（MLLM）推理引导的启发式搜索任务，在虚拟动作空间中迭代优化物体的位置和尺度，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体放置方法依赖手工规则或在有限数据集上的监督学习，限制了其泛化能力和可解释性，尤其在涉及新物体和新场景的开放世界场景中表现不佳。

Method: 将开放世界物体放置形式化为启发式搜索任务，由MLLM推理引导；在虚拟动作空间中迭代细化物体位置和尺度；采用由粗到精的搜索策略确保快速收敛；评估两种决策变体：Metric-guided Selection和MLLM-as-a-judge。

Result: 在多个基准测试上取得SOTA结果，尤其在未见过的开放世界场景中表现突出；人类研究表明MLLM-as-a-judge变体能产生比度量驱动方法更具感知连贯性的放置结果，揭示了标准评估指标与人类视觉判断之间的差距。

Conclusion: presto证明了利用MLLM推理进行零样本、无需训练的开放世界物体放置的有效性，并指出了现有评估指标与人类感知之间的不一致性，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Object placement is critical in image composition, requiring spatially and semantically coherent positioning of objects within diverse scenes. Existing approaches typically rely on hand-crafted rules or supervised learning on limited datasets, which restricts their generalization and interpretability, especially in open-world scenarios involving novel objects and scenes. In this work, we reformulate open-world object placement as a heuristic search task guided by reasoning from a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM). We introduce \textsf{presto}, a zero-shot, training-free framework that operates within an imaginary action space to iteratively refine object position and scale. Our coarse-to-fine search strategy ensures fast convergence, and we evaluate two decision-making variants: Metric-guided Selection and MLLM-as-a-judge. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that \textsf{presto}~achieves state-of-the-art performance, particularly in previously unseen, open-world settings. Human studies further reveal that the MLLM-as-a-judge variant produces more perceptually coherent placements than metric-driven approaches, highlighting a gap between standard evaluation metrics and human visual judgment.

</details>


### [47] [Semantic Slots for Video Object-Centric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21636)
*Khalil Sabri,Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau,Nicolas Saunier,Wassim Bouachir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SemanticSlots，一种视频目标中心学习（OCL）方法，其核心观点是传统方法瓶颈在于解码器而非编码器。通过使用基于Transformer的解码器利用图像上下文信息，解耦slot的空间锚定需求，使slot成为位置不变的语义查询，从而仅用单帧状态即可分解后续视频帧，无需复杂的时序预测器。在YouTube-VIS上，SemanticSlots较VideoSAUR的mBO提升31个点，较当前SOTA提升21个点，达到86.6% ARI和62.8% mBO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频目标中心学习研究主要聚焦于优化编码器架构以维持时序一致性，作者提出质疑并指出真正的瓶颈在于解码器——传统解码器迫使slot空间锚定，限制了其适应运动的能力，从而影响视频分解的质量。

Method: 提出SemanticSlots，采用基于Transformer的解码器来利用图像上下文信息，使slot摆脱边界精度和空间位置编码的负担，转化为本质上目标位置不变的语义查询。这些语义查询通过特征检索而非坐标记忆来匹配目标特征，且该属性使从单帧计算的slot即可分解后续视频帧，消除了对复杂时序预测器或辅助时序损失的需求。

Result: 在YouTube-VIS数据集上，SemanticSlots相比VideoSAUR在mBO上提升31个点，相比当前最先进方法提升21个点，达到86.6% ARI和62.8% mBO。

Conclusion: 验证了视频OCL的主要瓶颈在于解码器而非编码器这一观点，证明基于Transformer的解码器配合语义查询范式能够从根本上简化视频分解的时序建模，获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) has traditionally focused on refining the encoder architecture to ensure temporal consistency. In this paper, we argue that the primary bottleneck lies in the decoder. We show that traditional decoders force slots to be spatially anchored, hindering their ability to adapt to motion. We propose SemanticSlots, which uses a Transformer-based decoder that leverages image context, relieving slots from encoding boundary precision and spatial location. This allows slots to function as semantic queries that are inherently object position invariant, retrieving matching features rather than memorizing coordinates. More importantly, this property allows slots computed from a single frame to decompose subsequent video frames, eliminating the need for complex temporal predictors or auxiliary temporal losses. Results on YouTube-VIS show that SemanticSlots improves upon VideoSAUR by 31 points in mBO and outperforms current state-of-the-art methods by 21 points, achieving 86.6% ARI and 62.8% mBO.

</details>


### [48] [Spiking Neural Networks for Energy-Efficient Object Detection in Forward-Looking Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22072)
*Gwenevere Frank,Gert Cauwenberghs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次评估SNN在前视声呐（FLS）图像目标检测中的适用性。SpikeYOLO（全脉冲网络）在三个FLS数据集上以显著更低的理论计算能耗（最高降低4.4倍）达到与CNN基线（如YOLOv8m）相当的检测精度，并在斑点噪声鲁棒性上优于CNN，表明SNN是AUV能效受限场景下FLS目标检测的可行且节能的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV是能源受限平台，推进系统与计算/传感器争夺有限电池能量。AUV常运行在黑暗或浑浊水域，光学传感价值有限，声呐是主要感知方式。CNN虽在FLS目标检测中表现优异，但能耗高（YOLOv8m需322 mJ/推理）。SNN因稀疏累积操作和二进制脉冲激活在能效上极具潜力，且FLS回波的稀疏高对比结构在编码层面与脉冲编码天然匹配。然而此前无人评估SNN在FLS目标检测中的适用性，这是一个尚未探索的重要研究空白。

Method: 提出SpikeYOLO，一个使用替代梯度训练的完全脉冲网络，并在三个FLS目标检测数据集上将其与SOTA CNN基线进行基准对比，包括UATD、稀疏的Marine-Debris-FLS等。评估指标涵盖检测精度（mAP@0.5, mAP@0.5:0.95）、理论计算能耗以及针对乘性斑点噪声的鲁棒性（σ=0.4与σ=0.6噪声水平）。

Result: 在UATD上，SpikeYOLO T=2达到3.3倍更低的理论计算能耗（97 vs 322 mJ），精度具竞争力（0.529 mAP@0.5:0.95 vs YOLOv8m的0.575）。在稀疏的Marine-Debris-FLS数据集上，SpikeYOLO在mAP@0.5上与YOLOv8m持平，并以4.4倍更低能耗超越YOLO-SONAR和Fast R-CNN基线。鲁棒性方面，SpikeYOLO对乘性斑点噪声更鲁棒（σ=0.4时退化3.0% vs YOLOv8m的8.9%），在σ=0.6时甚至全面超越YOLOv8m。

Conclusion: SNN（SpikeYOLO）在FLS目标检测中是可行且能效显著优于CNN的替代方案，尤其在AUV这类功率受限平台上具有实际部署价值。其能耗优势与对斑点噪声更强的鲁棒性使其非常适合真实世界的FLS部署场景，为未来AUV嵌入式声学感知系统的节能设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are increasingly important tools in industries ranging from research, to energy, to defense. AUVs are power-constrained platforms operating in remote environments with fixed battery capacities, where propulsion competes with compute and sensors for power over lengthy mission durations. AUVs frequently operate in dark or turbid waters where optical sensing is of limited value, and rely on sonar as their primary sensing modality. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are the state-of-the-art solution for object detection in forward-looking sonar imagery, but are energy expensive (e.g. YOLOv8m: 322 mJ/inference). Spiking neural networks (SNNs) rely on binary spike activations and thus sparse accumulate-only operations, allowing them to be remarkably energy efficient, particularly when paired with dedicated neuromorphic hardware. The sparse, high-contrast structure of forward-looking sonar (FLS) returns is structurally matched to spike coding in a way that optical imagery is not. No prior work has assessed the suitability of SNNs for object detection in FLS imagery. SpikeYOLO, a fully spiking network trained with surrogate gradients, was benchmarked against state-of-the-art CNN baselines on three FLS object detection datasets. Key results: SpikeYOLO T=2 achieves 3.3$\times$ lower theoretical compute energy on UATD (97 vs 322 mJ) at competitive accuracy (0.529 mAP@0.5:0.95 vs. YOLOv8m's 0.575); SpikeYOLO matches YOLOv8m on mAP@0.5 and outperforms YOLO-SONAR and Fast R-CNN baselines on the sparse Marine-Debris-FLS dataset at 4.4$\times$ lower energy; SpikeYOLO demonstrates superior robustness to multiplicative speckle noise (3.0% degradation at $σ{=}0.4$ vs. 8.9% for YOLOv8m), outperforming YOLOv8m outright at $σ{=}0.6$, directly relevant to real-world FLS deployment.

</details>


### [49] [Spotter: Efficient Urban Visual Localization via Geo-Referenced Facade Landmarks in GPS-Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23290)
*Antoni Valls,Jordi Sanchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Spotter，一种实时视觉定位框架，利用建筑立面作为全局地理参考源，在离线阶段处理Google街景全景图构建紧凑度量数据库，运行时通过级联检索和几何验证实现全局相机定位，在巴塞罗那可穿戴智能眼镜数据集上超越了基于里程计的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集城市环境中，GPS信号经常因多径传播而退化；视觉里程计存在漂移问题；基于地图匹配的方法需要可靠的GPS先验且计算量大、无法在边缘设备实时运行。因此需要一种不依赖GPS的实时视觉定位方案。

Method: Spotter包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段：处理Google街景全景图，对建筑立面进行语义分割，并将多视图立体深度与制图数据配对，构建紧凑的度量数据库；(2) 运行时阶段：通过级联的检索和几何验证流程匹配查询图像，恢复细粒度的全局相机定位。同时保留在GPS可用时整合GPS信号的能力。

Result: 在巴塞罗那多个区域的智能眼镜行人序列新数据集上，Spotter优于基于里程计的基线方法，定位精度与最先进的基于地图的方法相当，同时运行帧率显著更高。

Conclusion: 建筑立面可作为可靠的城市环境全局地理参考源，Spotter在保持高帧率实时运行的同时实现了与SOTA地图方法相当的定位精度，为可穿戴和机器人平台在城市峡谷中的视觉定位提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate visual localization on robotic and wearable platforms remains challenging in dense urban environments. Existing methodologies typically rely on GPS for absolute positioning, yet GPS signals frequently degrade in urban canyons due to multipath propagation. Consequently, standard solutions like visual odometry suffer from unmitigated drift over time, while map-matching techniques struggle to acquire the reliable GPS priors they need, on top of being too computationally heavy for real-time edge execution. To address these limitations, we propose Spotter, a robuts and real-time visual localization framework that uses building facades as a reliable source of global geo-reference, while retaining the capability to integrate GPS signals when available. In an offline stage, Spotter processes Google Street View panoramas by semantically segmenting facades and pairing multi-view stereo depth with cartographic data to build a compact metric database. At runtime, query images are matched via a cascaded retrieval and geometric verification pipeline to recover fine-grained global camera localization. We benchmark Spotter on a newly collected dataset of pedestrian sequences acquired with wearable smart glasses across several districts of Barcelona. Experimental results show that Spotter outperforms odometry-based baselines and achieves localization accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art map-based methods while operating at significantly higher frame rates.

</details>


### [50] [WildHandBench: A Benchmark for Handwritten Text Understanding that Challenges MLLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22959)
*Jun Zhang,Qiao Zhao,Cheng Cui,Jianying Qu,Zhongkai Sun,Jianwen Yang,Changda Zhou,ZhuoXin Liu,Shubin Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WildHandBench基准，包含500份涵盖三种结构（自由文本、表格、公式）、四种语言和九种真实场景的手写文档，并引入Prior-Driven Error (PDE)指标来量化模型错误源自语言先验而非视觉证据的程度。评估18个SOTA模型后发现：最优模型仅达71.85%整体准确率，人类优于所有模型但差距很小（77.09% vs 71.85%），且63-91%的模型错误是先验驱动的，而人类仅49%，揭示了传统准确率指标无法捕捉的对语言先验的系统性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶尖模型在印刷文档解析上已达96.34%，但对手写文档的处理能力尚未被充分刻画。现有基准仅关注孤立的文本或公式，忽略了手写表格和真实世界退化现象，且仅报告聚合准确率而不解释模型失败的根本原因。

Method: 构建WildHandBench基准数据集（500份文档，覆盖三种结构、四种语言、九种真实场景），提出Prior-Driven Error (PDE)指标以区分错误源于语言先验还是视觉证据，并同时评估18个SOTA模型与经过校准的人类基线。

Result: 最优模型仅达到71.85%整体准确率；人类在所有模型之上但差距狭窄（77.09% vs 71.85%）；模型错误的63-91%为先验驱动，而人类仅为49%，表明模型对语言先验存在系统性依赖。

Conclusion: 现有模型在手写文档解析上与人类仍存在差距，且传统准确率指标掩盖了模型错误本质上的不同——模型过度依赖语言先验而非视觉证据，这需要新的评估维度和建模思路来推动手写文档解析的进步。

Abstract: While the top model on OmniDocBench now reaches 96.34% overall on printed-document parsing, the ability of current models to handle challenging handwritten documents remains largely uncharacterized. Existing benchmarks focus on isolated text or formulas, overlook handwritten tables and real-world degradation, and report aggregate accuracy without explaining why models fail.
  We present WildHandBench, a benchmark containing 500 handwritten documents across three structures (free text, tables, formulas), four languages, and nine real-world scenarios. We introduce a Prior-Driven Error (PDE) metric that quantifies whether errors originate from language priors rather than visual evidence. Evaluating 18 state-of-the-art models together with calibrated human baselines, we find: (1) the best model achieves only 71.85% overall; (2) humans outperform all models yet the gap is narrow (77.09% vs. 71.85%); and (3) model errors are qualitatively different from human errors -- 63-91% of model errors are prior-driven versus only 49% for humans, exposing systematic reliance on language priors that conventional accuracy metrics cannot capture.

</details>


### [51] [TASSO: TAsk-Specific Subspace Optimization for Continual Learning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21487)
*Chang Sun,Francesco Barbato,Matteo Caligiuri,Pietro Zanuttigh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TASSO，一种新的持续学习范式，通过子空间学习和几何感知知识蒸馏来同时缓解灾难性遗忘和零样本能力退化，在CLIP视觉语言模型的多领域任务增量学习和类增量学习基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）在持续学习场景中表现优异，但在持续适配过程中会同时出现灾难性遗忘和零样本能力退化问题，严重降低性能。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题，需要一种既能保留潜在空间几何结构又能保证网络可塑性的新范式。

Method: TASSO包含两个互补技术：1) 子空间学习——学习一系列任务特定的低秩投影器，在优化交叉熵之前对潜在表示进行投影，避免沿全嵌入维度的不必要参数更新；2) 几何感知知识蒸馏——使用基于测地线距离的损失函数，从先前任务模型中蒸馏知识，同时有效保留潜在空间的几何结构。这两种设计聚焦于任务特定的流形，提供强正则化。

Result: 在CLIP视觉语言模型的多领域任务增量学习（multi-domain task incremental learning）和类增量学习（class incremental learning）基准上，TASSO在缓解遗忘和保持零样本能力方面均优于现有最先进方法，取得了清晰的性能提升。

Conclusion: TASSO通过子空间学习和几何感知知识蒸馏相结合的方式，实现了对潜在空间几何结构的有效保留与网络可塑性的兼顾，证明该范式能显著同时减少灾难性遗忘和零样本退化，为VLMs的持续学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot capabilities, making them an attractive solution for continual learning across diverse tasks. However, during continual adaptation, both catastrophic forgetting and zero-shot degradation occur, severely degrading performance. In this paper, we introduce TASSO, a new paradigm that efficiently preserves the latent space geometry while ensuring network plasticity. We achieve this with two complementary techniques: subspace learning and geometry-aware knowledge distillation. Specifically, we first learn a sequence of task-specific low-rank projectors, which we use to project the latent representations before optimizing cross-entropy. Secondly, we employ a geodesic-distance-based loss that distills knowledge from the previous-task model while effectively preserving the latent space geometry. These design choices not only avoid unnecessary parameter updates along the full embedding dimensions but also improve learning by focusing on task-specific manifolds. Moreover, the geometry-aware distillation provides strong regularization and significantly reduces both catastrophic forgetting and zero-shot degradation throughout the continual learning sequence. Experimental results with the CLIP vision language model in the multi-domain task incremental and class incremental learning benchmarks demonstrate clear improvements over state-of-the-art methods in mitigating forgetting and preserving zero-shot capabilities.

</details>


### [52] [ViSMoE: Visual-Aware Sparse Mixture-of-Experts for Embodied Referring Expression Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21878)
*Shuo Feng,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViSMoE框架，通过将稀疏混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）与视觉感知路由策略相结合，为具身智能体处理视点（view）和物体（object）两种不同类型的视觉信息，从而获得判别性的视觉表征，在REVERIE和SOON数据集上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身指代表达定位（Embodied Referring Expression Grounding）任务中，智能体需要在每个步骤选择导航视点，并在目的地从候选物体中定位目标物体。然而现有方法大多使用vanilla视觉编码器统一处理视点和物体，未能区分这两类视觉信息，导致视点和物体的表征模糊不清，影响定位准确性。

Method: 提出ViSMoE框架，将稀疏混合专家（sparse Mixture-of-Experts）与视觉感知路由策略（visual-aware routing policy）相结合，针对视点和物体这两种不同类型的视觉输入进行专门处理，从而生成更具判别性的视觉表征。

Result: 在REVERIE和SOON两个标准数据集上的实验结果表明，ViSMoE的性能超越了以往的最先进方法（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过为视点和物体分别设计专属的专家路由机制，ViSMoE有效解决了统一视觉编码导致的表征歧义问题，验证了稀疏混合专家模型在具身视觉语言导航任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied Referring Expression Grounding is the task of enabling an agent to navigate in real environments and to localize a remote object based on natural language instructions. In this scenario, the agent needs to select one view for navigation at each step and identify a specific object among all candidate objects at the destination. However, most of the previous approaches fail to distinguish between views and objects, instead processing them using the vanilla vision encoder, which results in ambiguous representations of both views and objects. To address the above issues, we propose ViSMoE, which equips sparse Mixture-of-Experts with a visual-aware routing policy for the embodied agent. This framework processes different types of visual information specifically, resulting in discriminative visual representations for both views and objects. Experimental results on REVERIE and SOON datasets demonstrate that ViSMoE outperforms the previous state-of-the-art methods, showing the superiority of our proposed method.

</details>


### [53] [TransHands: Repurposing Human Pose Encoders as Hand Pose Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22341)
*Milo Piccioli,Gianluca Amprimo,Claudia Ferraris,Gabriella Olmo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TransHands，一个主干无关的迁移学习框架，利用大规模人体姿态数据中学习到的运动表示来提升3D手部姿态估计性能，解决了3D手部标注数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D手部姿态估计面临3D标注手部数据集规模小、多样性不足的瓶颈，而人体运动数据丰富；本文旨在通过将人体运动表示迁移到手部域来弥补这一数据缺口。

Method: TransHands采用两阶段训练与微调策略，结合轻量级手部特定输入适配模块，将手部运动学与预训练人体运动编码器的表示空间对齐，并支持多种主干架构（transformer、图神经网络、频域模型）。

Result: 在四种SOTA运动建模架构上，从人体姿态数据学习的运动先验均实现一致性精度提升，表现出强跨域泛化能力（尤其在具身/第一人称视角场景），并适用于真实世界下游任务。

Conclusion: TransHands验证了人体运动先验可跨架构、跨域地有效迁移到手部姿态估计任务，为在数据稀缺条件下训练手部模型提供了一种通用且高效的替代方案，无需从头训练手部专用生物力学模型。

Abstract: Lifting 3D hand poses from 2D monocular representations remains challenging due to the limited availability of large-scale, diverse 3D-annotated hand datasets, in contrast to the abundance of human body motion data. We address this limitation by transferring motion representations learned from large body pose corpora to the hand domain. We introduce TransHands, a backbone-agnostic transfer learning framework that enables pre-trained human motion encoders to be effectively adapted for 3D hand pose estimation from 2D pose inputs. Rather than training hand-specific biomechanical models from scratch, TransHands combines a two-stage training and fine-tuning strategy with a lightweight hand-specific input adaptation module that aligns hand kinematics with the representation space learned for full-body motion. We evaluate TransHands across four state-of-the-art motion modeling architectures, including transformer-based, graph-based, and frequency- domain models. Results demonstrate that motion priors learned from body pose data transfer consistently across architectures, yielding consistent accuracy gains, strong cross-domain generalization, particularly in challenging egocentric settings, and applicability for downstream tasks in real-world contexts.

</details>


### [54] [Hyper^2: Unleashing Hyperbolic Geometry's Full Potential via Dual-Space Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22238)
*Guantian Zheng,Haiyang Xu,Tianyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hyper^2，一种双空间一致性框架，通过将双曲几何同时应用于损失函数和编码器/注意力编码，解决了HyperbolicCD中"交叉几何不匹配"（损失是双曲的但编码器是欧几里得的）问题，在点云补全任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HyperbolicCD虽然开创性地将双曲几何引入点云补全，但仅将损失改为双曲形式而编码器仍为欧几里得，导致梯度通过链式法则在回传前就被平均消除，造成位置相关的监督信息丢失，性能提升有限（仅3-7%）。作者旨在找出并解决这个真正的瓶颈。

Method: 提出两个模型无关的指标来量化交叉几何不匹配：特征-损失相关性r_FL和有效梯度利用率u_G。在此基础上提出Hyper^2框架：(1) 保留HyperbolicCD的双曲Chamfer损失；(2) 将相同的arcosh(1+alpha d^2)函数形式作为位置偏置注入refinement注意力的双曲距离编码，使编码器和损失共享同一曲率alpha。两个操作符均为欧几里得距离上的O(N log N)标量非线性函数。

Result: 在SVDFormer基线上，Hyper^2在ShapeNet-55上实现-22.9%的Chamfer距离改善，远超单空间消融的线性叠加(-13.2%)；在21个未见过的ShapeNet-34类别上达到-37.5%。两个指标仅在编码器和损失同时为双曲时从(0.68, 39%)跃升至(0.95, 87%)，计算开销仅增加约1.6% FLOPs。

Conclusion: 几何一致性（而非单一操作符）是双曲监督在点云补全中发挥效用的关键。编码器与损失的双曲一致性使梯度信息得以有效传播，是实现双曲几何在点云补全中显著增益的必要条件。

Abstract: HyperbolicCD pioneered hyperbolic geometry for point cloud completion by replacing the Euclidean Chamfer distance with arcosh(1+alpha||x-y||^2), but the reported gains are modest (3-7% Chamfer reduction across SeedFormer, PointAttN and PMP-Net backbones on PCN and ShapeNet-55). We argue the bottleneck lies elsewhere: the loss is hyperbolic but the encoder it back-propagates through is Euclidean, so the position-dependent supervision of the loss is averaged away by the chain rule before it reaches the parameters. We call this a cross-geometry mismatch, and make it testable through two model-agnostic indicators, feature-loss correlation r_FL and effective gradient utilisation u_G. On an SVDFormer backbone trained with HyperbolicCD's loss alone we measure (r_FL, u_G) = (0.68, 39%). We propose Hyper^2, a dual-space consistency framework that extends HyperbolicCD by reusing the identical arcosh(1+alpha d^2) functional form as a positional bias on the refinement attention (a hyperbolic distance encoding), paired with HyperbolicCD's hyperbolic Chamfer loss under a single shared curvature alpha. Both operators are O(N log N) scalar non-linearities on Euclidean distances and together add only ~1.6% FLOPs over SVDFormer. Hyper^2 delivers -22.9% Chamfer on ShapeNet-55 over SVDFormer (well above the 13.2% linear sum of the -12.0% loss-only and -1.2% encoding-only single-space ablations) and -37.5% on the 21 unseen ShapeNet-34 categories. The two indicators remain essentially flat for any single-space configuration but jump together to (0.95, 87%) only when both encoder and loss are hyperbolic, supporting the claim that geometric consistency across encoder and loss, rather than either operator alone, is what enables hyperbolic supervision in point cloud completion. Code is available at https://github.com/Ethan-Zheng136/Hyper-2.

</details>


### [55] [AI-based worker guidance in assembly and disassembly operations using multimodal ego/exo-centric data capture and structured task knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22617)
*Vivek Chavan,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的从专家演示中提取结构化任务知识的方法，利用第一人称和第三人称视频及叙述的多模态信息，生成程序性文档和上下文感知的工人指导，并在真实拆解案例上验证了超越静态图像方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配和拆解流程依赖难以文档化、复用和转移的专家知识，现有的静态图像方法无法充分捕捉程序性结构和执行上下文，亟需一种能够从专家演示中自动提取结构化任务知识的方法。

Method: 采用数据驱动方法，联合编码来自视频（第一人称与第三人称）和叙述的时间与多模态信息，推导出结构化任务表示，以支持程序性文档生成和上下文感知的工人指导。

Result: 在真实拆解案例研究中验证了方法的有效性，视频表示相比静态图像方法能更好地捕捉程序性结构和执行上下文，展示了第一人称视频理解在维修、培训和循环制造中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 基于视频的多模态方法能从专家演示中实现结构化任务知识的自动提取，对维修、培训和循环制造等应用具有重要价值，为解决专家知识难以文档化和转移的行业痛点提供了数据驱动的可行方案。

Abstract: Assembly and disassembly processes rely on expert knowledge that is difficult to document, reuse, and transfer. This paper presents a data-centric approach for extracting structured task knowledge from expert demonstrations using egocentric and exocentric recordings. Temporal and multimodal information from video and narration is jointly encoded to derive structured task representations that enable procedural documentation and context-aware worker guidance. The approach is evaluated on a real-world disassembly case study, demonstrating that video-based representations capture procedural structure and execution context beyond static image-based methods. The results highlight the potential of egocentric video understanding for repair, training, and circular manufacturing applications. Project website: https://indego-assistant.github.io/

</details>


### [56] [DamageScope: Vision-Language Retrieval at Scale for Disaster Damage Assessment from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21529)
*Ravi K. Rajendran,Biplob Debnath,Murugan Sankaradas,Srimat T. Chakradhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DamageScope是一个基于检索增强生成（RAG）框架的系统，结合卫星图像、视觉语言模型（VLM）和大语言模型（LLM）来自动化自然灾害后的财产损失评估。该系统引入多向量嵌入聚类算法（索引时间降低14倍）和双存储数据架构（减少LLM API调用，响应延迟降低约3倍），在可扩展性和运营效率之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的灾区财产损失评估依赖人工现场检查，成本高、耗时且存在安全风险。虽然卫星图像和VLM技术可以实现远程评估，但将VLM集成到大规模地球观测管线中面临计算效率、数据组织和信息检索三方面的挑战。需要一种既能处理大规模数据又保持高效率和低成本的可扩展解决方案。

Method: DamageScope采用RAG框架，主要包含两个技术创新：(1) 多向量嵌入聚类算法，优于传统单向量嵌入方法，将索引时间减少14倍；(2) 双存储数据架构，通过优化数据组织和缓存策略来最小化LLM API调用次数，降低运营成本和响应延迟。系统从卫星图像中提取结构化视觉表示，支持交互式自然语言查询进行损失评估。

Result: 该框架在可扩展性和运营效率方面取得显著提升：多向量嵌入聚类算法将索引时间减少高达14倍，双存储架构将运营成本和响应延迟降低约3倍。系统能够在保证评估准确性的同时有效处理大规模数据。

Conclusion: DamageScope通过RAG框架有效地将VLM和LLM整合到大规模地球观测流程中，在可扩展性和运营效率之间取得了平衡，为实际灾害损失评估任务提供了稳健且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Timely and accurate assessment of property damage is critical following natural disasters. Traditional on-site inspections are labor-intensive, costly, and often pose safety risks. Advances in satellite imagery and vision-language models (VLMs) enable scalable remote damage assessment; however, integrating VLMs into large-scale Earth observation pipelines presents challenges in computational efficiency, data organization, and information retrieval. To address these challenges, we present DamageScope, a retrieval-augmented framework that combines satellite imagery with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate property damage analysis. Built on a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework, DamageScope extracts structured visual representations from satellite imagery to support interactive natural language queries for damage assessment. To address scalability, we introduce a novel multi-vector embedding-based clustering algorithm that outperforms traditional single-vector embedding approaches while reducing indexing time by up to 14x. Furthermore, a dual-store data architecture minimizes LLM API calls, reducing both operational cost and response latency by up to approximately 3x. By effectively balancing scalability and operational efficiency, DamageScope provides a robust and practical solution for real-world damage assessment tasks.

</details>


### [57] [MLLM-Assisted Audio VOS: A 3rd Place Report for the MeViS-Audio Track, 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23234)
*Liangtao Shi,Jinxia Xie,Xiantao Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无需训练的音频引导视频目标分割框架，通过集成多模态大语言模型与SAM分割模型，在MeViS-Audio赛道取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频引导的视频目标分割需要跨模态理解音频、文本与视觉信息，但现有方法通常需要大量任务特定训练。作者希望探索能否通过组合现有基础模型（MLLM与SAM）实现这一任务，而无需额外的模型训练或微调。

Method: 将音频引导视频目标分割任务分解为多个阶段，每个阶段选配合适的基础模型：利用MLLM强大的多模态推理能力建模文本-视觉对应关系（将音频信息转化为文本语义），并采用基于SAM的分割模型生成精确的目标掩码。全程无需额外训练或任务特定微调。

Result: 该框架在第八届LSVOS挑战赛的MeViS-Audio赛道中取得了有竞争力的性能，验证了利用基础模型进行音频引导视频分割的有效性。

Conclusion: 训练免费的基础模型组合方案能够有效地完成音频引导视频目标分割任务，证明了领域无关基础模型在复杂跨模态任务中的潜力。

Abstract: In this technical report, we present a training-free framework for audio-guided video object segmentation, which integrates Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with SAM-based segmentation models. We decompose the task into several stages and identify suitable foundation models for each stage. Without introducing additional model training or task-specific fine-tuning, our approach leverages the strong multimodal reasoning capabilities of MLLMs to model text-visual correspondence and employs SAM-based models for accurate object mask generation. The proposed framework demonstrates the effectiveness of leveraging foundation models for audio-guided video segmentation and achieves competitive performance in the MeViS-Audio Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge.

</details>


### [58] [Robust Global Structure-from-Motion via View Graph Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22054)
*Jiamin Xu,Lixing Yao,Weichen Dai,Renshu Gu,Zunjie Zhu,Weiwei Xu,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子图引导的视图图剪枝框架，用于鲁棒的全局结构从运动（SfM）估计，通过利用可靠子图的内部一致性来识别并移除不可靠的连接边，从而提高相机位姿估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局SfM虽然比增量SfM具有更好的可扩展性，但其性能高度依赖于视图图中边的质量，视觉模糊匹配导致的错误边会引发相机注册失败和重建伪影，因此需要一种能够有效识别和移除不可靠边的方法。

Method: 该方法首先将视图图划分为局部一致的子图，在每个子图内执行全局SfM以获得可靠的相机位姿；然后跨子图应用基于RANSAC的边剪枝来移除不一致的边；最后在优化后的视图图上执行全局SfM。

Result: 在模糊、序列化和无序图像数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在挑战性条件下提高了全局SfM的鲁棒性。进一步的神经渲染评估显示，改进的相机估计带来了更高质量的新视图合成结果。

Conclusion: 子图引导的剪枝框架能够有效利用局部一致性信息增强全局SfM的鲁棒性，同时改善下游任务（如神经渲染）的质量。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion (SfM) aims to estimate camera poses and reconstruct 3D structures from a collection of unordered images. Compared with incremental SfM, global SfM achieves better scalability by jointly estimating camera poses based on a view graph constructed from pairwise correspondences. However, its performance is highly sensitive to erroneous edges caused by visually ambiguous matches, which may lead to incorrect camera registration and reconstruction artifacts. In this work, we propose a subgraph-guided view graph pruning framework for robust global SfM. Our key idea is to exploit the internal consistency of reliable subgraphs to identify and remove unreliable connections. Specifically, we first partition the view graph into locally consistent subgraphs and perform global SfM within each subgraph to obtain reliable camera poses. We then apply RANSAC-based edge pruning across subgraphs to remove inconsistent edges, and finally perform global SfM on the refined view graph. Extensive experiments on ambiguous, sequential, and unordered image datasets demonstrate that our method improves the robustness of global SfM under challenging conditions. Further evaluation with neural rendering shows that the improved camera estimation leads to higher-quality novel view synthesis results.

</details>


### [59] [Simplified Cross-Modal Calibration for Heterogeneous Event-RGB Stereo Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22965)
*Nico Hessenthaler,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无运动的跨模态标定框架，利用在标准消费级显示器上呈现的时间调制混合ChArUco靶标，实现事件相机与帧相机的外参标定，无需运动、无需精确同步，且比现有方法分别降低44%和6%的平均重投影误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机与帧相机组成的异质立体系统的精确外参标定仍是实际瓶颈。现有方法通常需要传感器或靶标运动、精确同步或昂贵的event-to-image重建，限制了其实用性。因此需要一种简单、免运动的跨模态标定方法。

Method: 在标准消费级显示器上呈现时间调制且混合的ChArUco靶标。通过交替显示原始图案与部分混合版本，靶标既能可靠触发事件，又能被帧相机持续观测，避免空白帧，并将同步要求降低为粗粒度的触发对齐。将事件离散为与RGB图像粗对齐的帧，进行轻量级去噪，然后基于ChArUco完成内参和双目外参标定。

Result: 大量实验评估了混合不透明度、显示亮度、外部光照、视角和手持采集等条件下的鲁棒性。相比最强的基于运动的方法（E2Calib + Kalibr）和一种非运动基准（Plasberg et al.），本方法平均重投影误差分别降低44%和6%，同时显著简化标定流程。在机器人眼手标定案例中展示了实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种简单、免运动、轻同步依赖的跨模态标定方案，在精度和易用性上均优于现有方法，并在真实机器人场景中验证了实用性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic calibration between event-based and frame-based cameras remains a practical bottleneck for heterogeneous stereo systems. Existing approaches often require sensor or target motion, precise synchronization, or computationally expensive event-to-image reconstruction. We propose a simple, motion-free cross-modal calibration framework that uses a temporally modulated, blended ChArUco target presented on standard consumer displays. By alternating between the original pattern and a partially blended version, the target reliably triggers events while remaining continuously observable to a frame-based camera, avoiding blank frames and reducing synchronization constraints to a coarse, trigger-based alignment. We discretize events into frames coarsely aligned with the RGB images, apply lightweight denoising, and perform ChArUco-based intrinsic and stereo extrinsic calibration. Extensive experiments assess robustness to blending opacity, display brightness, external illumination, viewing angle, and handheld acquisition. Compared to the strongest motion-based reference (E2Calib + Kalibr) and a non-motion-based reference (Plasberg et al.), our approach reduces the mean reprojection error by $44\%$ and $6\%$, respectively, while substantially simplifying the calibration procedure. Finally, we demonstrate practical utility in a robotic eye-to-hand calibration case study, showing consistent transformations and stable downstream geometric measurements even under partial occlusions. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/nhessenthaler/simple-evrgb-cal.

</details>


### [60] [VIG: Visual Information Gain as a Reward Signal for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21883)
*Wen Luo,Xiaohan Yi,Xiaotao Huang,Liqun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIG（视觉信息增益），一种信息论GRPO奖励方法，通过两次前向传递（有/无图像）计算每个推理token的图像信息增益，用于压缩多模态大语言推理模型的CoT，在六个基准和多个模型规模上提升准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大推理模型常产生冗长的CoT轨迹，其中大量token（如重复视觉描述、自反思、非视觉填充词）不贡献答案却增加推理成本。现有CoT压缩方法只优化输出长度，从不衡量推理token是否真正基于图像锚定，缺乏信息论层面的grounding度量。

Method: 提出VIG奖励：利用GRPO框架，对每个推理token计算图像降低其预测不确定性的信息增益。在线计算，通过对同一策略做两次前向传递（一次带图像、一次不带图像）得到，无需参考链、外部标注或辅助奖励模型。

Result: 在六个主要多模态推理基准和三个Qwen3-VL-Thinking模型规模（2B/4B/8B）上，加上8B上的R1-Onevision-Bench评估，VIG一致地改善了准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 高效的多模态推理源于提升视觉信息密度——每个推理token通过锚定图像来证明自身价值，而非通过施加长度预算。核心主张是"每一个推理token都应通过锚定图像来赢得其位置"。

Abstract: Multimodal large reasoning models often rely on long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces in which a substantial fraction of tokens, such as repeated visual descriptions, self-reflection, and other visually-disengaged filler, inflate inference cost without contributing to the answer. Existing CoT compression methods optimize output length but never measure whether a reasoning token is actually grounded in the image. We propose \textbf{VIG} (Visual Information Gain), an information-theoretic GRPO reward that scores each reasoning token by how much the image reduces its predictive uncertainty. VIG is computed online from two forward passes of the same policy, one with and one without the image, so no reference chains, external annotations, or auxiliary reward models are needed. Across six main multimodal reasoning benchmarks and three Qwen3-VL-Thinking model sizes (2B/4B/8B), plus an additional R1-Onevision-Bench evaluation on 8B, VIG consistently improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off, supporting our central claim: \emph{efficient multimodal reasoning emerges from raising visual information density, where every reasoning token earns its place by anchoring to the image, rather than from imposing a length budget.} Our source code is available at https://github.com/chaser682/vig.

</details>


### [61] [What Remains Normal? Clean Images Miss Useful Near-Defect Normal Patches for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23299)
*Joongwon Chae,Runming Wang,Peiwu Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于记忆的异常检测器中，记忆构建方式（覆盖选择器）与训练集稀疏污染之间的关系。作者提出CLEANCON，一种基于袋外交叉图像的支持门控策略，在不改变表示、记忆大小、构建器和推理规则的条件下改变候选图像资格。实验表明全局覆盖严重过度表征稀疏污染，而CLEANCON将最终记忆污染降至接近零，并在全部12组匹配比较中提升类别宏平均P-AP。然而沿保留度扫描发现，污染最低的记忆并非P-AP最高，性能随污染上升仍持续改进，说明记忆污染程度不能作为排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于记忆的异常检测器在选择训练块存入记忆时，没有单独检查几何稀有性是否使其值得信任，导致记忆可能被稀疏污染数据污染。作者希望探究记忆覆盖选择与训练污染之间的耦合关系，并提出无需改变其他组件的去污染方案。

Method: 在固定表示和记忆预算下，比较随机、质心（medoid）、局部和全局四种覆盖选择器；提出CLEANCON——一种基于袋外（out-of-bag）交叉图像的支持门控机制，在保持表示、绝对记忆大小、构建器和推理规则不变的前提下，改变候选图像是否可进入记忆的资格判定。

Result: 全局覆盖选择器严重过度表征稀疏污染；CLEANCON将最终记忆中的污染降至接近零，并在全部12组匹配比较中提升类别宏平均P-AP。但沿保留度（retention）扫描中发现，污染最低的记忆未取得最高P-AP，性能在污染上升时仍继续改进。

Conclusion: 记忆中的污染程度并不能决定最终记忆的P-AP排序，即更低的污染并不必然带来更高的检测性能。这表明在异常检测记忆构建中，"污染的减少"与"性能的提升"之间存在非单调关系，需要更精细地权衡覆盖与污染之间的张力。

Abstract: Memory-based anomaly detectors store nominal training patches and score test patches against this memory. A patch selected for coverage therefore becomes a nor- mal reference without a separate check that geometric rarity makes it safe to trust. We probe this coupling with sparse training contamination. Under fixed representa- tions and memory budgets, we compare random, medoid, local, and global coverage selectors. We then use CLEANCON, an out-of-bag cross-image support gate that changes candidate-image eligibility while fixing the representation, absolute mem- ory size, builder, and inference rule. Global coverage strongly over-represents sparse contamination. CLEANCON reduces final-memory contamination to approx- imately zero and increases category-macro P-AP in all 12 matched comparisons. Yet along a retention sweep, the lowest-contamination memory does not attain the highest P-AP; performance continues to improve while contamination rises. Mem- ory contamination therefore does not order the resulting memories by P-AP.Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jw-chae/cleancon.

</details>


### [62] [Improved denoising diffusion probabilistic models with efficient non-diagonal covariance modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21972)
*Rui Xia,Ayan Das,Artem Artemev,Andi Zhang,Guillaume Hennequin,Alberto Bernacchia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kronecker-DCT (K-DCT)的协方差模型，用于加速DDPM采样。通过利用Kronecker分解捕捉颜色通道间相关性，并在DCT频域建模空间协方差，将计算复杂度从二次降为对数线性，在少量去噪步数下实现了优于现有SOTA采样器的FID和似然性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDPM采样可通过利用去噪后验协方差的二阶信息来加速，但现有方法仅使用对角线等简化协方差近似，无法捕捉自然图像中强大的非对角线像素间和相关颜色通道间的相关性，以及慢衰减的幂律频谱结构。

Method: 提出K-DCT协方差模型：采用Kronecker分解分别建模颜色间协方差和空间协方差，其中空间协方差在DCT频域中建模。DCT的使用将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n log n)，每个去噪步骤的计算和内存开销可忽略不计。使用预训练的分数模型在CIFAR-10、Celeb-A、ImageNet和LSUN数据集上学习K-DCT结构化的去噪后验协方差摊销。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、Celeb-A、ImageNet和LSUN数据集上的实验表明，K-DCT方法在FID和似然两个方面均优于先前SOTA去噪采样器，尤其在少量去噪步数的场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: 通过更忠实地建模自然图像统计结构（跨颜色通道相关性和频域空间相关性），K-DCT协方差模型能够在保持极低计算开销的同时，显著提升少步数DDPM采样的质量。

Abstract: The sampling process of Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPMs) can be accelerated by leveraging second-order information in the form of approximations to the denoising posterior covariance -- allowing samples of acceptable quality to be produced in fewer but larger sampling steps. Previous attempts at using such information have used drastic (e.g.\ diagonal) simplifications of the covariance. These do not do justice to the peculiar statistical structure of natural images, which exhibit strong non-diagonal correlations between pixels and color channels, and a slow-decaying power-law frequency spectrum. Here, we develop a novel covariance model that captures these features. Our Kronecker-DCT (K-DCT) model uses a Kronecker-factored decomposition of inter-color covariances and spatial covariances modeled in the frequency domain using the Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT). The use of the DCT reduces the computational complexity from quadratic to log-linear, resulting in negligible computational and memory overhead in each denoising step. By learning K-DCT-structured amortizations of the denoising posterior covariance using pre-trained score models on CIFAR-10, Celeb-A, ImageNet and LSUN datasets, we show improved performance compared to previous SOTA denoising samplers, both in terms of FID and likelihoods, especially in the regime of few denoising steps.

</details>


### [63] [Learning Implicit Constitutive Laws for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22102)
*Xiaoyang Liu,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GCA是一个从单目动态视频中学习3D高斯表示的可变形物体隐式本构定律的框架，通过LoRA适配、基于秩的深度几何锚定（RDGA）和本构先验正则化（CPR）两个对齐模块，在合成、真实到仿真及真实数据集上优于现有方法，合成基准上Chamfer距离比最强基线低48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式方法在噪声监督下容易陷入局部最小值且缺乏物理可解释性，而显式方法依赖预定义本构方程、泛化性有限且在单目设置下不稳定。作者旨在从单目动态视频中仅依靠几何初始化学习物理动力学，克服上述局限。

Method: GCA框架包含两个关键对齐模块：(1) Rank-based Depth-Geometric Anchors (RDGA) 通过尺度不变的基于秩的深度对齐建立稳健几何约束，减少对不可靠像素级颜色监督的依赖；(2) Constitutive Prior Regularizer (CPR) 将经典本构模型作为软可微先验引入，正则化优化过程同时保留隐式建模的灵活性，即使实际材料不在假设集合中也能工作。两者与LoRA适配统一在3D高斯表示框架中。

Result: 在合成、真实到仿真和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，GCA优于现有方法，合成基准上Chamfer距离比最强基线降低48%，且在单目监督下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GCA通过融合几何对齐和物理先验正则化，有效解决了单目动态视频中学习隐式本构定律面临的局部最小值和可解释性挑战，证明了基于秩的深度几何约束在单目物理学习中的有效性。

Abstract: We present GCA (Gaussian Constitutive Alignment), a framework for learning implicit constitutive laws from monocular dynamic video of deformable objects represented by 3D Gaussians. Given a static multi-view scan for geometric initialization, our method learns intrinsic physical dynamics solely from a single fixed-viewpoint video of the moving object. Existing implicit methods often suffer from local minima under noisy supervision and lack physical interpretability, while explicit approaches rely on predefined constitutive equations, limiting generalizability and becoming unstable in monocular settings. To address these challenges, our framework unifies LoRA-based adaptation with two key alignment modules. First, we propose Rank-based Depth-Geometric Anchors (RDGA) to establish robust geometric constraints from monocular dynamic observations via scale-invariant rank-based depth alignment, reducing the reliance on unreliable pixel-level color supervision. Second, a Constitutive Prior Regularizer (CPR) integrates classical constitutive models as soft differentiable priors, regularizing the optimization while preserving the flexibility of implicit modeling---even when the actual material is absent from the hypotheses. Extensive experiments on synthetic, real-to-sim, and real-world datasets demonstrate that GCA outperforms existing methods, achieving 48% lower Chamfer Distance than the strongest baseline on synthetic benchmarks while remaining robust under monocular supervision.

</details>


### [64] [SiZeUp: Fast 3D Proxy from Aerial Images via Depth Ordinal Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22821)
*Wenjun Zhou,Yunshan Li,Qiaoyu Zhu,Weidan Xiong,Hao Zhang,Daniel Cohen-Or,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SiZeUp，一种从倾斜航空影像快速构建大规模3D城市代理模型的方法。采用高度从足迹表示将3D建筑抽象降为低维优化问题，引入序数深度一致性损失，通过可微渲染器将代理模型映射为多视图深度图像，利用单目深度模型的相对深度先验进行监督。相比现有方法实现23-52倍加速，同时保持相当的覆盖率和体积一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模城市场景的3D代理模型（建筑足迹拉伸为高度）构建需求日益增长，但现有管道通常依赖密集点云重建或显式特征匹配，计算成本高昂、速度缓慢，难以扩展到大规模城市建模场景。

Method: 采用高度-足迹（height-from-footprint）参数化表示，将建筑足迹按单一高度参数拉伸，把3D建模降为低维优化问题。提出序数深度一致性损失，通过可微渲染器将参数化代理模型渲染为多视图深度图，与单目深度模型预测的深度先验在相对深度排序上对齐；利用序数而非度量深度，规避单目尺度模糊问题。结合动态视图选择策略加速优化。

Result: 在代理级覆盖率和体积一致性相当的条件下，比现有最先进代理重建管道快23-52倍，适合大规模城市建模。

Conclusion: 序数深度一致性损失配合可微渲染提供了一种无需密集点云重建和显式特征匹配的稳定、高效高度估计方案，在速度与精度之间取得了良好平衡，可用于大规模城市场景建模。

Abstract: We present SiZeUp, a fast and scalable approach for constructing large-scale 3D urban proxy models directly from calibrated oblique aerial imagery. Our method adopts a height-from-footprint representation, reducing 3D building abstraction to a low-dimensional optimization problem in which building footprints are extruded by a single height parameter. To enable efficient and robust height estimation, we introduce an ordinal depth consistency loss that enforces agreement between the relative depth ordering of rendered proxies and depth priors predicted by a monocular depth model. This is realized through a differentiable renderer that maps parametric building proxies into multi-view depth images, allowing gradients to be propagated from depth supervision to building heights. Our ordinal formulation produces stable optimization in practice and avoids explicit feature matching or dense point cloud reconstruction. Rather than relying on metric depth, which can be unreliable under monocular scale ambiguity, our ordinal depth consistency loss operates on relative depths, providing a more reliable signal across views. Combined with an efficient dynamic view selection, our approach achieves a 23-52$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art proxy reconstruction pipelines while maintaining comparable proxy-level coverage and volume consistency, making it well suited for large-scale urban modeling tasks.

</details>


### [65] [VISTA: Test-Time Compositional Alignment for Visual Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22521)
*Hossein Shahabadi,Niki Sepasian,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是首个用于next-scale视觉自回归（VAR）图像生成的基于梯度的测试时对齐框架，通过冻结Transformer优化中间表示来引导视觉预测满足组合约束（如属性绑定和空间关系），无需修改模型参数或额外训练。在Infinity模型上，VISTA在2B和8B骨干网络上分别将目标组合类别平均得分提升近20%和6%，且保持图像质量，2B模型+VISTA甚至超越了其四倍规模的骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V AR模型和扩散模型一样存在组合性失败（compositional failures），即生成的图像违反提示中的属性绑定和空间关系。虽然扩散模型已有丰富的测试时对齐方法，但next-scale VAR生成的"有状态、离散、多分辨率采样"过程使现有技术无法适用，因此需要为VAR开发专门的测试时对齐框架。

Method: VISTA基于Infinity构建，直接在生成过程中干预：通过冻结的Transformer优化中间表示，将视觉预测朝组合约束方向引导。它引入了跨尺度的稳定优化机制，并提供一个可扩展的目标空间，任何可微分的cross-attention约束都可以插入。全程不修改模型参数，也不需要额外训练。

Result: 在两个基准和两个模型规模上，VISTA在所有目标组合类别上均有提升：2B骨干网络平均目标得分提升近20%，8B骨干网络提升近6%，空间关系类别提升最大。独立偏好模型评分显示输出质量几乎提升了20%（VISTA从未优化该指标）。2B模型+VISTA超越了其四倍规模的骨干模型。

Conclusion: 测试时对齐可以恢复模型规模之间相当大一部分组合能力的差距，表明VISTA是一种有效的、无需训练即可提升VAR模型组合生成能力的方法，且不影响图像质量。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive (VAR) models have emerged as a fast, high-quality alternative to diffusion for text-to-image generation, but like diffusion models they exhibit persistent compositional failures, producing images that violate the attribute bindings and spatial relations specified in the prompt. While a rich line of test-time alignment methods has developed for diffusion, no comparable approach exists for next-scale VAR generation, whose stateful, discrete, multi-resolution sampling process makes existing techniques inapplicable. We close this gap with \textbf{VISTA} (\textbf{Vi}sual Autoregressive \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{T}est-time \textbf{A}lignment), the first gradient-based test-time alignment framework for next-scale autoregressive image generation. Built on Infinity, VISTA intervenes directly in the generation process, optimizing intermediate representations through the frozen transformer to steer visual predictions toward compositional constraints, without modifying model parameters or requiring additional training. VISTA introduces the mechanisms needed to make such optimization stable across scales, together with an extensible objective space that any differentiable constraint on cross-attention can plug into. Across two benchmarks and two model scales, VISTA improves every targeted compositional category, raising the mean targeted score by nearly 20\% on a 2B backbone and almost 6\% on an 8B backbone, with the largest gains on spatial relations. Image quality is preserved: an independent preference model VISTA never optimizes scores its outputs nearly 20\% higher. Notably, the 2B model with VISTA surpasses a backbone four times its size, indicating that a substantial part of the compositional gap between model scales is recoverable at test time.

</details>


### [66] [GaussVid: Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting with 3D-Aware Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21849)
*Xinhui Liu,Can Wang,Wei Jiang,Wei Wang,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种3D感知的视频修复框架，通过相机条件和边界锚定几何先验来增强稀疏视角3DGS重建质量，在像素、结构保真度和多视角一致性上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下的3DGS重建常出现明显伪影。虽然视频扩散模型能提供时空先验，但直接微调用于修复时缺乏多相机几何感知，导致多视角不一致。

Method: 构建大规模3DGS视频数据集进行专门微调；采用首尾帧作为边界锚点，编码对应相机关系，将相机条件的几何先验显式注入视频生成管线，引导网络进行几何基础的修复。

Result: 在视频先验修复方法中，所提方法在PSNR/SSIM上达到最佳像素和结构级保真度，改善多视角一致性，并在LPIPS感知质量上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 边界锚定、相机感知的先验能够桥接2D视频生成与3D多视角约束之间的差距，有效提升稀疏3DGS重建的质量与多视角一致性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has achieved remarkable success in novel view synthesis; however, reconstructions under sparse views often exhibit noticeable artifacts. While recent video diffusion models provide strong spatio-temporal priors for 3DGS restoration, directly fine-tuning them for restoration is suboptimal, as they lack awareness of the underlying multi-camera geometry, resulting in multi-view inconsistencies. In this work, we propose a novel 3D-aware video restoration framework designed to enhance the quality of sparse 3DGS reconstruction. Specifically, we construct a large-scale 3DGS video dataset to enable specialized fine-tuning. To bridge the gap between 2D video generation and 3D multi-view constraints, we introduce a camera-conditioned geometric prior. By using the first and last frames as boundary anchors and encoding the corresponding camera relationships, we explicitly inject spatial structure into the video generation pipeline. This boundary-anchored, camera-aware prior guides the network toward geometrically grounded restoration that remains coherent across viewpoints. Extensive experiments show that, among video-prior restoration methods, our approach attains the best pixel- and structure-level fidelity (PSNR/SSIM) and improves multi-view consistency, while remaining competitive in perceptual quality (LPIPS).

</details>


### [67] [MIVIFI: Bridging Perspective and Fisheye Domains for Training Multi-View Fisheye Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23140)
*Matthias Neuwirth-Trapp,Begüm Altunbas,Jiayi Wang,Yan Xia,Maarten Bieshaar,Xinyu Huang,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次正式提出"基于体积语义表示的多视角鱼眼图像生成"这一新问题，并提出两种方法：SyntheOcc-FE（直接适配SyntheOcc架构）和MIVIFI（利用等距柱状投影进行跨域学习，结合KITTI-360鱼眼图像与nuScenes多视角标准图像），以解决鱼眼数据集稀缺带来的生成泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶视觉感知系统需要360°全覆盖，鱼眼相机以低成本实现全周环绕。然而现有鱼眼多视角数据集有限，合成罕见角case通常依赖昂贵的3D仿真；生成模型虽在标准透视图像上成功，但在广角畸变领域的应用仍属空白。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) SyntheOcc-FE：将SyntheOcc架构适配到鱼眼数据；(2) MIVIFI：基于跨域学习，利用KITTI-360鱼眼图像和nuScenes多视角标准图像，通过等距柱状投影（Equirectangular Projections）桥接数据集域间差距，实现场景内容的高保真操纵，包括修改语义占用输入以引入/消除特定对象，并渲染数据集中缺失的气象和光照条件。

Result: 定量与定性实验表明两种方法均能实现鲁棒的照片级多视角鱼眼图像生成，且跨域策略MIVIFI在处理数据稀缺问题上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 跨域学习策略（MIVIFI）能够有效缓解鱼眼数据稀缺的瓶颈，实现高质量、可操控的多视角鱼眼图像合成，为自动驾驶鱼眼视觉感知训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Achieving 360° coverage is critical for the visual perception systems of autonomous vehicles. Fisheye cameras offer a cost-effective solution by enabling full surround coverage with as few as two sensors. However, existing multi-view fisheye datasets are limited, and synthesizing rare corner cases typically requires computationally expensive 3D simulations, hindering the training. While generative models have achieved significant success in standard perspective imagery, their application to wide-angle distortion remains unexplored. In this work, we formally introduce the novel problem of multi-view fisheye image generation conditioned on volumetric semantic representations and present two distinct methods. We first propose SyntheOcc-FE, which adapts the SyntheOcc architecture to fisheye data. While effective, this method is constrained by the scarcity of fisheye datasets, which limits its generalization. To overcome these limitations, we propose our second method, MIVIFI (multi-view fisheye), which leverages cross-domain learning with Equirectangular Projections. By bridging the gap between dataset domains using KITTI-360 fisheye images alongside nuScenes multi-view standard images, our approach enables high-fidelity manipulation of scene content. This framework enables the structural modification of semantic occupancy inputs to introduce or eliminate specific actors and facilitates the rendering of diverse meteorological conditions and illumination scenarios absent in the limited fisheye datasets. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our methods achieve robust photorealistic multi-view fisheye image generation and highlight the specific advantages of our cross-domain strategy for handling data scarcity.

</details>


### [68] [Thinking Beyond Videos: Unifying Video Reasoning and Deep Research for Open-World Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23329)
*Wenqi Liu,Shijie Ma,Yunxiao Wang,Meng Liu,Qile Su,Han Liu,Bohan Hou,Xuanyu Zheng,Changyi Liu,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Kaiyu Jiang,Yingxin Li,Jiankang Chen,Xu Wang,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Jianhua Yin,Yinwei Wei,Xuemeng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoRover，一个统一的视频深度研究框架，通过迭代协调视频裁剪、多模态搜索和网页浏览来应对开放世界视频理解中需要稀疏视觉证据与外部知识的挑战。配套构建了自动数据整理流水线（26K SFT轨迹和3K RL实例）以及VideoRover-Bench基准，实验证明VideoRover-8B-RL在直接回答场景中达到与专有模型相当的性能，并优于使用相同工具集的更大开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界视频理解要求模型既能定位稀疏的视觉证据，又能获取视频及参数化记忆中缺失的外部知识。然而"Thinking-with-Videos"（主动时间感知）和"Deep Research"（多步信息检索）这两种关键能力通常被孤立开发，缺乏将它们统一整合的框架。

Method: VideoRover采用迭代式动作选择机制，根据每个工具（视频裁剪、多模态搜索、网页浏览）的结果决定下一步动作，使局部视频片段指导外部检索，外部证据反过来触发进一步的视频检验和验证。同时构建了自动数据整理流水线，生成26K经过验证的SFT轨迹和3K具有挑战性的RL实例，并设计了按视频时长和研究难度分层的VideoRover-Bench基准。

Result: 在VideoDR和VideoRover-Bench上的实验表明，VideoRover-8B-RL在无需工具的直接回答设定下性能与专有模型相当，同时在使用相同工具套件的情况下优于更大的开源模型。消融研究和训练动态分析验证了主动视频定位、外部检索和长程强化学习之间的互补作用。

Conclusion: VideoRover验证了将主动视频感知与多步外部信息检索统一到单一框架的有效性，揭示了视频定位、外部检索和长程强化学习三者互补的协同关系，为开放世界视频深度研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Open-world video understanding often requires a model to locate sparse visual evidence and acquire external knowledge that is absent from the video and its parametric memory. While Thinking-with-Videos enables active temporal perception and Deep Research supports multi-step information seeking, the two capabilities are typically developed in isolation. We introduce VideoRover, a unified Video Deep Research framework that iteratively coordinates video cropping, multimodal search, and webpage browsing. Given a video-question pair, VideoRover uses each tool result to select the next action, so localized video clips guide external retrieval and retrieved evidence triggers further video inspection and verification. To develop this capability, we construct an automated data curation pipeline, producing 26K verified SFT trajectories and 3K challenging RL instances. We also introduce VideoRover-Bench, a benchmark stratified by video duration and research difficulty. Experiments on VideoDR and VideoRover-Bench show that our VideoRover-8B-RL achieves performance comparable to proprietary models in the direct-answer setting without tool use while outperforming larger open-source models equipped with the same tool suite. Ablation studies and training dynamics further validate the complementary roles of active video grounding, external retrieval, and long-horizon reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [69] [Hyperbolic Hierarchical Clustering for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22665)
*Jianan Wei,Guikun Chen,Zhiyuan Weng,Chunchao Guo,Yujia Wang,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ClusterMixer，一种基于层次聚类的可解释token混合器，在双曲空间中进行聚类以建模视觉数据的树状层级关系，并构建HCFormer骨干网络，在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割和语义分割等任务上均优于同类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉骨干网络中的token mixer（基于卷积、注意力、MLP或其混合）虽然能平衡精度与计算成本，但本质上是黑盒模型，编码过程缺乏可解释性。作者希望通过回归机器学习中最经典的聚类方法，设计一种透明、可解释的token混合机制。

Method: ClusterMixer将token混合过程显式地建模为层次聚类机制，并在双曲空间中执行聚类以低失真地嵌入视觉数据固有的树状层级关系。基于ClusterMixer构建HCFormer骨架，并设计了系列精细的聚类策略以保证跨任务的鲁棒性能。

Result: 大量实验表明，HCFormer在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割和语义分割等多个任务上持续优于同类骨干网络。

Conclusion: HCFormer结合了透明性与有效性，有望推动视觉骨干网络向可解释方向实现范式转变。

Abstract: We investigate the token mixer in vision backbones by revisiting clustering, one of the most classic approaches in machine learning.
